# 总统演讲：Chunks 积累

取自五位总统的 19 篇官方实录，按演讲分组。记录可迁移的搭配、话语套语和句子骨架；正式与演讲语气标在意思中，不把出现次数当作日常高频或掌握证据。

原文见 [总统演讲原文合集](../原文与参考资料/原文、教材与影视/总统演讲原文合集：GovInfo.md)。以下是候选积累；段号对应本地可读层，原句逐字保留。

## 1993-01-20 · 克林顿：1993 年就职演说

### 1. bring forth sth.

**意思：** 带来／产生某事物；较正式（正式）

> This ceremony is held in the depth of winter, but by the words we speak and the faces we show the world, we force the spring, a spring reborn in the world's oldest democracy that brings forth the vision and courage to reinvent America.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 强调某种能力、想法或结果的出现，不是字面拿出。

### 2. triumph over sth.

**意思：** 战胜困难或强大对手（正式）

> And I thank the millions of men and women whose steadfastness and sacrifice triumphed over depression, fascism, and communism.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 比克服更有胜利色彩。

### 3. assume responsibility (for sth.)

**意思：** 承担新的责任（正式）

> Today, a generation raised in the shadows of the cold war assumes new responsibilities in a world warmed by the sunshine of freedom but threatened still by ancient hatreds and new plagues.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** assume在此是承担，不是猜测。

### 4. stagnant wages

**意思：** 停滞不涨的工资（中性）

> Raised in unrivaled prosperity, we inherit an economy that is still the world's strongest but is weakened by business failures, stagnant wages, increasing inequality, and deep divisions among our own people.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 经济与职场搭配。

### 5. deep divisions

**意思：** 严重分歧／深层裂痕（中性）

> Raised in unrivaled prosperity, we inherit an economy that is still the world's strongest but is weakened by business failures, stagnant wages, increasing inequality, and deep divisions among our own people.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 可描述团队或社会内部冲突。

### 6. take the oath

**意思：** 宣誓／宣誓就职（正式）

> When George Washington first took the oath I have just sworn to uphold, news traveled slowly across the land by horseback and across the ocean by boat.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 宣誓承诺履行职责，不是随意许愿。

### 7. earn sb.'s livelihood

**意思：** 谋生／获得生计（正式）

> We earn our livelihood in America today in peaceful competition with people all across the Earth.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 比earn a living稍郑重。

### 8. face hard truths

**意思：** 正视令人难受但真实的事实（中性）

> We know we have to face hard truths and take strong steps, but we have not done so; instead, we have drifted.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 不能把不想听的意见自动称为事实。

### 9. shake sb.'s confidence

**意思：** 动摇某人的信心（中性）

> And that drifting has eroded our resources, fractured our economy, and shaken our confidence.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** shake在此为心理影响。

### 10. from time to time

**意思：** 不时／偶尔（中性）

> Thomas Jefferson believed that to preserve the very foundations of our Nation, we would need dramatic change from time to time.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 不表示持续不断。

### 11. muster the determination to do sth.

**意思：** 鼓起决心做某事（正式）

> From our Revolution to the Civil War, to the Great Depression, to the civil rights movement, our people have always mustered the determination to construct from these crises the pillars of our history.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 12. There is nothing wrong with A that cannot be cured by B.

**意思：** A的问题都可以借B来解决；强烈积极判断框架（演讲）

> There is nothing wrong with America that cannot be cured by what is right with America.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 是修辞性整体判断，不是一般事实保证；注意双重否定。

### 13. pledge an end to sth.

**意思：** 承诺结束某种局面（正式）

> And so today we pledge an end to the era of deadlock and drift, and a new season of American renewal has begun.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** pledge比一般打算更郑重。

### 14. provide for sb.

**意思：** 供养／保障某人的需要（中性）

> We must provide for our Nation the way a family provides for its children.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 不是仅仅把东西提供给某人。

### 15. We can do no less.

**意思：** 我们至少也应该做到这样／不能做得更少（正式）

> We can do no less.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 以前面的榜样或责任作为最低标准。

### 16. break the habit of doing sth.

**意思：** 改掉做某事的习惯（中性）

> It is time to break the bad habit of expecting something for nothing from our Government or from each other.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 后接动名词。

### 17. something for nothing

**意思：** 不付出代价就得到好处（中性）

> It is time to break the bad habit of expecting something for nothing from our Government or from each other.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 常用来批评只想白拿、不想承担。

### 18. bear responsibility

**意思：** 承担责任（中性）

> Posterity is the world to come: the world for whom we hold our ideals, from whom we have borrowed our planet, and to whom we bear sacred responsibility.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 19. pay sb.'s way

**意思：** 承担自己的费用／靠自己支付开销（中性）

> Powerful people maneuver for position and worry endlessly about who is in and who is out, who is up and who is down, forgetting those people whose toil and sweat sends us here and pays our way.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句说民众的劳动支付政府运转开销，和个人自费相关。

### 20. shout down sb./sth.

**意思：** 用喊声压过某人的声音／阻止其发言（中性）

> And so I say to all of you here: Let us resolve to reform our politics so that power and privilege no longer shout down the voice of the people.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 可字面指会场，也可比喻权势压制。

### 21. put sth. aside

**意思：** 暂时放下个人利益（正式）

> Let us put aside personal advantage so that we can feel the pain and see the promise of America.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 强调为共同目标不先顾私利。

### 22. since the dawn of sth.

**意思：** 自某事出现／开始以来（正式）

> This beautiful Capital, like every capital since the dawn of civilization, is often a place of intrigue and calculation.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 23. meet challenges

**意思：** 应对挑战（中性）

> To renew America, we must meet challenges abroad as well as at home.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** meet不是碰面。

### 24. shrink from sth.

**意思：** 因畏惧而回避某事（正式）

> While America rebuilds at home, we will not shrink from the challenges nor fail to seize the opportunities of this new world.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 常用于责任、困难或行动。

### 25. seize an opportunity

**意思：** 抓住机会（中性）

> While America rebuilds at home, we will not shrink from the challenges nor fail to seize the opportunities of this new world.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 表示及时利用，而不是只注意到。

### 26. be testament to sth.

**意思：** 是某事的明证／体现（正式）

> The brave Americans serving our Nation today in the Persian Gulf, in Somalia, and wherever else they stand are testament to our resolve.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 常见形式也有be a testament to。

### 27. raise sb.'s voice

**意思：** 发声／明确表达立场（中性）

> You have raised your voices in an unmistakable chorus.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 这里不只是提高音量，而是参与公共表达。

### 28. cast sb.'s vote

**意思：** 投票／作出自己的选择（中性）

> You have cast your votes in historic numbers.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** cast与vote的惯常搭配。

### 29. change the face of sth.

**意思：** 改变某事物的面貌（中性）

> And you have changed the face of Congress, the Presidency, and the political process itself.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 通常指重大、明显的变化。

### 30. undertake a mission

**意思：** 承担一项任务（正式）

> But no President, no Congress, no Government can undertake this mission alone.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 31. play sb.'s part

**意思：** 尽自己的一份责任／发挥应有作用（中性）

> My fellow Americans, you, too, must play your part in our renewal.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 可用于团队协作，不只指演戏。

### 32. act on sb.'s ideals

**意思：** 将理念付诸行动（中性）

> I challenge a new generation of young Americans to a season of service: to act on your idealism by helping troubled children, keeping company with those in need, reconnecting our torn communities.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** act on指依据某种想法采取行动。

### 33. keep company with sb.

**意思：** 陪伴某人／与某人为伴（中性）

> I challenge a new generation of young Americans to a season of service: to act on your idealism by helping troubled children, keeping company with those in need, reconnecting our torn communities.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 这里强调陪伴需要帮助的人；其他语境也可带交往意味。

### 34. in need

**意思：** 处于困难中／需要帮助（中性）

> I challenge a new generation of young Americans to a season of service: to act on your idealism by helping troubled children, keeping company with those in need, reconnecting our torn communities.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 常用作后置修饰或指需要帮助的人群。

### 35. give of oneself

**意思：** 奉献自己的时间、精力或能力（正式）

> There is so much to be done; enough, indeed, for millions of others who are still young in spirit to give of themselves in service, too.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 不是单纯把自己送出去。

### 36. young in spirit

**意思：** 精神上仍然年轻／充满活力（中性）

> There is so much to be done; enough, indeed, for millions of others who are still young in spirit to give of themselves in service, too.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 37. rededicate oneself to sth.

**意思：** 再次投入／重申对某事的承诺（正式）

> We rededicate ourselves to the very idea of America, an idea born in revolution and renewed through two centuries of challenge; an idea tempered by the knowledge that, but for fate, we, the fortunate, and the unfortunate might have been each other; an idea ennobled by the faith that our Nation can summon from its myriad diversity the deepest measure of unity; an idea infused with the conviction that America's long, heroic journey must go forever upward.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 常用于共同事业、长期使命。

### 38. but for sth.

**意思：** 若不是某个因素／要不是……（正式）

> We rededicate ourselves to the very idea of America, an idea born in revolution and renewed through two centuries of challenge; an idea tempered by the knowledge that, but for fate, we, the fortunate, and the unfortunate might have been each other; an idea ennobled by the faith that our Nation can summon from its myriad diversity the deepest measure of unity; an idea infused with the conviction that America's long, heroic journey must go forever upward.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 本句暗含如果命运不同，双方处境可能互换。

### 39. begin anew

**意思：** 重新开始（正式）

> And so, my fellow Americans, as we stand at the edge of the 21st century, let us begin anew with energy and hope, with faith and discipline.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1993-book1/html/PPP-1993-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 可用于新阶段或经历挫折后的重新出发。

## 1997-01-20 · 克林顿：1997 年就职演说

### 1. set sb.'s sights on sth.

**意思：** 把目标定在某事上（中性）

> Guided by the ancient vision of a promised land, let us set our sights upon a land of new promise.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 原句upon为较郑重变体；不因换介词就另收。 原段for decades to comes疑似原文误写；保留引文但不当标准接法。

### 2. on the edge of sth.

**意思：** 处于某个转折点的边缘／即将进入（演讲）

> It is our great good fortune that time and chance have put us not only at the edge of a new century, in a new millennium, but on the edge of a bright new prospect in human affairs, a moment that will define our course and our character for decades to comes.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 这里是接近新机遇，不是情绪即将崩溃。 原段for decades to comes疑似原文误写；保留引文但不当标准接法。

### 3. on the world stage

**意思：** 在国际舞台上（正式）

> Then, in turmoil and triumph, that promise exploded onto the world stage to make this the American Century.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** stage为公共影响范围的比喻。

### 4. long for sth.

**意思：** 渴望某事物（中性）

> America became the world's mightiest industrial power, saved the world from tyranny in two World Wars and a long cold war, and time and again reached out across the globe to millions who, like us, longed for the blessings of liberty.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** long作动词，与时间长无关。

### 5. in old age

**意思：** 在年老时／晚年（中性）

> Along the way, Americans produced a great middle class and security in old age, built unrivaled centers of learning and opened public schools to all, split the atom and explored the heavens, invented the computer and the microchip, and deepened the wellspring of justice by making a revolution in civil rights for African-Americans and all minorities and extending the circle of citizenship, opportunity, and dignity to women.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 常用于养老、生活保障。

### 6. be upon sb.

**意思：** 即将来临／已经逼近（正式）

> Now, for the third time, a new century is upon us and another time to choose.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 常见主语为时刻、节日、变化。

### 7. from coast to coast

**意思：** 从一端到另一端／横跨全国（中性）

> We began the 19th century with a choice: to spread our Nation from coast to coast.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 在美国通常指东西两海岸间；其他场景须有合适范围。

### 8. harness sth. to sth.

**意思：** 使某种力量为某目标服务（正式）

> We began the 20th century with a choice: to harness the industrial revolution to our values of free enterprise, conservation, and human decency.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 不是只字面驾驭动物。

### 9. unleash the potential of sb.

**意思：** 释放某人的潜力（中性）

> At the dawn of the 21st century, a free people must now choose to shape the forces of the information age and the global society, to unleash the limitless potential of all our people, and yes, to form a more perfect Union.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 常用于发展、教育或组织讨论。

### 10. made all the difference

**意思：** 起了决定性作用／使结果大不相同（中性）

> Those choices made all the difference.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 11. set a clear course

**意思：** 确定清晰的方向／路线（中性）

> We vowed then to set a clear course to renew our Nation.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 可用于项目、个人发展，不一定行船。

### 12. seem destined to do sth.

**意思：** 似乎注定要……（中性）

> Problems that once seemed destined to deepen, now bend to our efforts.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** seem保留说话者的不确定，destined加强趋势感。

### 13. strong enough to …, flexible enough to …

**意思：** 既足够强大能……，又足够灵活能……（正式）

> Our Founders understood that well and gave us a democracy strong enough to endure for centuries, flexible enough to face our common challenges and advance our common dreams in each new day.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 平行评价两种能力；保留各自补语，不写成万能固定格言。

### 14. live within sb.'s means

**意思：** 量入为出／不超出收入水平花钱（中性）

> We need a new Government for a new century, humble enough not to try to solve all our problems for us but strong enough to give us the tools to solve our problems for ourselves, a Government that is smaller, lives within its means, and does more with less.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 可用于家庭、个人或机构预算。

### 15. do more with less

**意思：** 用更少资源做更多事情（中性）

> We need a new Government for a new century, humble enough not to try to solve all our problems for us but strong enough to give us the tools to solve our problems for ourselves, a Government that is smaller, lives within its means, and does more with less.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 强调资源效率，不自动保证质量。

### 16. stand up for sth.

**意思：** 捍卫某种价值、利益或立场（中性）

> Yet where it can stand up for our values and interests around the world, and where it can give Americans the power to make a real difference in their everyday lives, Government should do more, not less.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 不是站起来。

### 17. humble enough not to …, but strong enough to …

**意思：** 足够谦逊而不……，又足够强大能……；对照两种能力（正式）

> We need a new Government for a new century, humble enough not to try to solve all our problems for us but strong enough to give us the tools to solve our problems for ourselves, a Government that is smaller, lives within its means, and does more with less.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 18. responsible citizenship

**意思：** 负责任的公民行为／公民责任（正式）

> Our Founders taught us that the preservation of our liberty and our Union depends upon responsible citizenship.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 公共事务词组。

### 19. take time out to do sth.

**意思：** 从自己的生活安排中腾出时间做某事（中性）

> There is work to do, work that Government alone cannot do: teaching children to read, hiring people off welfare rolls, coming out from behind locked doors and shuttered windows to help reclaim our streets from drugs and gangs and crime, taking time out of our own lives to serve others.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 强调主动留出时间。

### 20. come together / come apart

**意思：** 凝聚在一起／分裂瓦解（中性）

> Will we all come together, or come apart?

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 本句描述共同体，二者并列构成对照。

### 21. cloaked in sth.

**意思：** 披着某种幌子／以某事作伪装（正式）

> Prejudice and contempt cloaked in the pretense of religious or political conviction are no different.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 披着某物的外衣；本句pretense指幌子，不把整个修饰链称为固定短语。

### 22. feel at home with sb.

**意思：** 与某人在一起感到自在、融洽（中性）

> And we shall replace them with the generous spirit of a people who feel at home with one another.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 不同于对某个主题很熟悉的at home with sth.。

### 23. forge new ties

**意思：** 建立新的联系／关系（正式）

> Great rewards will come to those who can live together, learn together, work together, forge new ties that bind together.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** forge在此是建立，不是伪造。

### 24. close at hand

**意思：** 近在眼前／很快可能实现（中性）

> Cures for our most feared illnesses seem close at hand.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 本句seem限制预测强度。

### 25. build bonds with sb.

**意思：** 与某人建立联系／纽带（中性）

> Instead, now we are building bonds with nations that once were our adversaries.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 不专指金融债券。

### 26. the world over

**意思：** 全世界／遍及世界（中性）

> Growing connections of commerce and culture give us a chance to lift the fortunes and spirits of people the world over.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 固定副词性说法。

### 27. lift sb.'s spirits

**意思：** 让某人心情振奋／使人精神一振（中性）

> Growing connections of commerce and culture give us a chance to lift the fortunes and spirits of people the world over.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 28. within reach

**意思：** 在可获得／可达到的范围内（中性）

> The knowledge and power of the information age will be within reach not just of the few but of every classroom, every library, every child.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 可指机会、资源、目标或物品距离。

### 29. meet sb.'s obligations

**意思：** 履行义务／承担应尽责任（中性）

> Our land of new promise will be a nation that meets its obligations, a nation that balances its budget but never loses the balance of its values, a nation where our grandparents have secure retirement and health care and their grandchildren know we have made the reforms necessary to sustain those benefits for their time, a nation that fortifies the world's most productive economy even as it protects the great natural bounty of our water, air, and majestic land.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** meet不是遇见义务。

### 30. balance sb.'s budget

**意思：** 使预算收支平衡（中性）

> Our land of new promise will be a nation that meets its obligations, a nation that balances its budget but never loses the balance of its values, a nation where our grandparents have secure retirement and health care and their grandchildren know we have made the reforms necessary to sustain those benefits for their time, a nation that fortifies the world's most productive economy even as it protects the great natural bounty of our water, air, and majestic land.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 这里不提供财务建议，仅为搭配。

### 31. realize sb.'s full potential

**意思：** 充分发挥潜力（中性）

> Fellow citizens, let us build that America, a nation ever moving forward toward realizing the full potential of all its citizens.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** realize在此是实现，而非意识到。

### 32. be no match for sth.

**意思：** 比不上某物／无法与之抗衡（中性）

> In the end, all the world's wealth and a thousand armies are no match for the strength and decency of the human spirit.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 可比较能力、力量或影响。

### 33. have yet to do sth.

**意思：** 还没有做某事／尚待完成（中性）

> But let us never forget, the greatest progress we have made and the greatest progress we have yet to make is in the human heart.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 34. treat sb. as an equal

**意思：** 把某人当作平等的人来对待（中性）

> Like a prophet of old, he told of his dream that one day America would rise up and treat all its citizens as equals before the law and in the heart.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** as后接角色或身份。

### 35. live out sb.'s creed

**意思：** 践行自己的信念／把信条落实于生活（正式）

> His quest is our quest: the ceaseless striving to live out our true creed.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** creed偏郑重，可用beliefs在其他语境表达近似意思。

### 36. return sb. to office

**意思：** 再次选举某人任职（正式）

> The American people returned to office a President of one party and a Congress of another.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 不是把人送回办公室。

### 37. come to an end

**意思：** 结束／走到终点（中性）

> For all of us are on that same journey of our lives, and our journey, too, will come to an end.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-1997-book1/html/PPP-1997-book1-doc-pg43.htm)

**补充：** 可用于旅程、合作、阶段。

## 2001-01-18 · 克林顿：告别讲话

### 1. be grateful to sb. for sth.

**意思：** 由衷、非常感激某人（正式）

> I am profoundly grateful to you for twice giving me the honor to serve, to work for you and with you to prepare our Nation for the 21st century.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 比普通thanks更郑重。

### 2. rise to a challenge

**意思：** 迎接挑战并作出有力应对（中性）

> This has been a time of dramatic transformation, and you have risen to every new challenge.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** rise to不是把挑战升高。

### 3. the social fabric

**意思：** 社会联系与秩序构成的整体（正式）

> You have made our social fabric stronger, our families healthier and safer, our people more prosperous.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** fabric比喻人群、制度、关系组成的结构。

### 4. steer sb.'s course by sth.

**意思：** 以某种原则为自己的行动导航（正式）

> I have steered my course by our enduring values: opportunity for all, responsibility from all, a community of all Americans.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 以航行为比喻，可用于人生或组织方向。

### 5. put people first

**意思：** 把人放在首位／优先考虑人的需要（中性）

> I have sought to give America a new kind of Government, smaller, more modern, more effective, full of ideas and policies appropriate to this new time, always putting people first, always focusing on the future.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 可用于服务或组织理念。

### 6. break records

**意思：** 打破纪录（中性）

> Our economy is breaking records with more than 22 million new jobs, the lowest unemployment in 30 years, the highest homeownership ever, the longest expansion in history.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** record此处为最高或最低记录，不是文件。

### 7. lift sb. out of poverty

**意思：** 帮助某人摆脱贫困（正式）

> More than 3 million children have health insurance now, and more than 7 million Americans have been lifted out of poverty.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 常用被动形式，行动者由语境决定。

### 8. across the board

**意思：** 全面地／在各个方面或群体中（中性）

> Incomes are rising across the board.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不只在董事会里。

### 9. in every corner of the globe

**意思：** 遍及全球每个角落（正式）

> America has been a force for peace and prosperity in every corner of the globe.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 泛指广泛范围，带演讲夸张。

### 10. turn over the reins to sb.

**意思：** 把领导权／控制权交给某人（正式）

> I'm very grateful to be able to turn over the reins of leadership to a new President with America in such a strong position to meet the challenges of the future.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** reins是缰绳的领导权比喻。

### 11. leave sb. with … thoughts

**意思：** 临别时留给某人几个想法／建议（中性）

> Tonight I want to leave you with three thoughts about our future.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 用于结束发言或总结。

### 12. fiscal responsibility

**意思：** 财政纪律／负责任的预算管理（正式）

> First, America must maintain our record of fiscal responsibility.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 公共政策搭配。

### 13. pay down debt

**意思：** 逐步偿还、降低债务余额（中性）

> Through our last four budgets we've turned record deficits to record surpluses, and we've been able to pay down $600 billion of our national debt--on track to be debt-free by the end of the decade for the first time since 1835.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不等于一次全部付清。

### 14. on track to do sth.

**意思：** 按目前进展有望做到某事（中性）

> Through our last four budgets we've turned record deficits to record surpluses, and we've been able to pay down $600 billion of our national debt--on track to be debt-free by the end of the decade for the first time since 1835.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 表示轨迹或趋势，不是绝对保证。

### 15. provide tax relief

**意思：** 提供税收减免／减轻税负（正式）

> If we choose wisely, we can pay down the debt, deal with the retirement of the baby boomers, invest more in our future, and provide tax relief.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 公共政策搭配。

### 16. look to sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 期待、依靠某人去做某事（中性）

> People all around the world look to America to be a force for peace and prosperity, freedom and security.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 区别于仅朝某方向看。

### 17. be subject to sth.

**意思：** 受到某种影响、约束或风险（正式）

> But the forces of integration that have created these good opportunities also make us more subject to global forces of destruction, to terrorism, organized crime and narcotrafficking, the spread of deadly weapons and disease, the degradation of the global environment.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不只表示某门科目的subject。

### 18. close the gap between A and B

**意思：** 缩小A与B之间的差距（中性）

> The expansion of trade hasn't fully closed the gap between those of us who live on the cutting edge of the global economy and the billions around the world who live on the knife's edge of survival.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 可描述技能、收入或表现差距。

### 19. on the cutting edge of sth.

**意思：** 处于某领域最前沿（中性）

> The expansion of trade hasn't fully closed the gap between those of us who live on the cutting edge of the global economy and the billions around the world who live on the knife's edge of survival.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不是切东西的边缘。

### 20. on the knife's edge of sth.

**意思：** 处于某种危险局面的边缘（演讲）

> The expansion of trade hasn't fully closed the gap between those of us who live on the cutting edge of the global economy and the billions around the world who live on the knife's edge of survival.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 本句指生存岌岌可危，属强烈比喻。 不把完整on the knife's edge of sth.断言为日常高频固定形式。

### 21. It requires more than A; it requires B.

**意思：** 光有A还不够，还需要B（中性）

> This global gap requires more than compassion; it requires action.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 用递进修正“只有态度足够”的想法。

### 22. a powder keg

**意思：** 一触即发的危险局面／潜在危机（正式）

> Global poverty is a powder keg that could be ignited by our indifference.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 原指火药桶，此处为比喻。

### 23. If we want …, then we must …

**意思：** 要实现某目标，就必须采取某种行动（中性）

> If we want the world to embody our shared values, then we must assume a shared responsibility.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 24. achieve sb.'s aims

**意思：** 实现目标（中性）

> If the wars of the 20th century, especially the recent ones in Kosovo and Bosnia, have taught us anything, it is that we achieve our aims by defending our values and leading the forces of freedom and peace.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** aims偏目的、目标，不是瞄准动作。

### 25. in word and deed

**意思：** 在言语和行动上都……（正式）

> We must embrace boldly and resolutely that duty to lead--to stand with our allies in word and deed and to put a human face on the global economy, so that expanded trade benefits all peoples in all nations, lifting lives and hopes all across the world.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 强调说与做一致。

### 26. put a human face on sth.

**意思：** 让某个抽象过程体现人的处境和需要（正式）

> We must embrace boldly and resolutely that duty to lead--to stand with our allies in word and deed and to put a human face on the global economy, so that expanded trade benefits all peoples in all nations, lifting lives and hopes all across the world.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不只是给某物贴上人脸。

### 27. unite around common values

**意思：** 围绕共同价值团结起来（正式）

> As we become ever more diverse, we must work harder to unite around our common values and our common humanity.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** around连接共同的中心或目标。

### 28. overcome sb.'s differences

**意思：** 克服彼此分歧（中性）

> We must work harder to overcome our differences, in our hearts and in our laws.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 不表示个性差异必须被消灭，具体看语境。

### 29. regardless of sth.

**意思：** 不论某事／不受该因素影响（中性）

> We must treat all our people with fairness and dignity, regardless of their race, religion, gender, or sexual orientation, and regardless of when they arrived in our country--always moving toward the more perfect Union of our Founders' dreams.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 后接名词或从句，与despite有区别。

### 30. wish sb. sb.'s very best

**意思：** 向某人致以最诚挚的祝愿（正式）

> Hillary, Chelsea, and I join all Americans in wishing our very best to the next President, George W. Bush, to his family and his administration, in meeting these challenges, and in leading freedom's march in this new century.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 常用于告别或交接。

### 31. sb.'s best days lie ahead

**意思：** 最好的日子还在前面（演讲）

> As for me, I'll leave the Presidency more idealistic, more full of hope than the day I arrived, and more confident than ever that America's best days lie ahead.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 积极愿景，不是可验证的未来保证。

### 32. more … than ever

**意思：** 比以往任何时候都更……（中性）

> As for me, I'll leave the Presidency more idealistic, more full of hope than the day I arrived, and more confident than ever that America's best days lie ahead.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 原演讲语境；可按原意迁移，不因出现一次宣称高频。

### 33. In the years ahead, …

**意思：** 在未来的岁月里……（中性）

> In the years ahead, I will never hold a position higher or a covenant more sacred than that of President of the United States.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 比next year覆盖更宽时间。

### 34. There is no … I will … more … than …

**意思：** 没有什么……比……更令我……；最高评价框架（正式）

> But there is no title I will wear more proudly than that of citizen.

**来源：** [Bill Clinton · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2000-book3/html/PPP-2000-book3-doc-pg2952.htm)

**补充：** 保持比较对象和被评价维度；原例表达身份自豪。

## 2001-01-20 · 小布什：2001 年就职演说

### 1. the peaceful transfer of authority

**意思：** 权力的和平交接（正式／公共事务）

> The peaceful transfer of authority is rare in history, yet common in our country.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 属于制度论述搭配，不声称日常高频。

### 2. with grace

**意思：** 从容得体地；有风度地（中性偏正式／评价）

> As I begin, I thank President Clinton for his service to our Nation, and I thank Vice President Gore for a contest conducted with spirit and ended with grace.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句竞选结束时保持风度，不是舞蹈优雅。

### 3. be honored and humbled to do sth.

**意思：** 能做某事感到荣幸与谦卑（正式／感谢）

> I am honored and humbled to stand here where so many of America's leaders have come before me, and so many will follow.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 适合承接重任或荣誉，非受羞辱。

### 4. deserve a chance

**意思：** 值得得到一次机会（中性／公平）

> The grandest of these ideals is an unfolding American promise that everyone belongs, that everyone deserves a chance, that no insignificant person was ever born.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 可用于普通人或新方案，不仅政治立场。

### 5. be called to do sth.

**意思：** 被赋予做某事的使命或责任（正式／演讲）

> Americans are called to enact this promise in our lives and in our laws.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 此处非打电话叫来，而是道德责任。

### 6. take root

**意思：** 扎根；开始稳固发展（中性／过程比喻）

> Now it is a seed upon the wind, taking root in many nations.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句信念在各国扎根。

### 7. pass sth. along

**意思：** 将某物传下去／转交下去（中性）

> It is the inborn hope of our humanity, an ideal we carry but do not own, a trust we bear and pass along.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 a trust；不单指消息转发。

### 8. have a long way yet to travel

**意思：** 尚有很长一段路要走（中性／过程）

> Even after nearly 225 years, we have a long way yet to travel.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句社会理想仍未完成，是旅程比喻。

### 9. run deep

**意思：** 根深蒂固；十分深刻（中性／分析）

> And sometimes our differences run so deep, it seems we share a continent but not a country.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 differences；可接 feelings、divisions 等。

### 10. It seems 从句

**意思：** 看起来似乎……（中性／推断）

> And sometimes our differences run so deep, it seems we share a continent but not a country.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句省略 that，不将语气判断当证实事实。

### 11. While 从句, others 从句

**意思：** 有些人……，另一些人却……（论述／对照）

> While many of our citizens prosper, others doubt the promise, even the justice of our own country.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** while 在此对比不同群体，非单纯同时。

### 12. in sb.'s reach

**意思：** 在某人能力所及范围内（中性／信心）

> I know this is in our reach because we are guided by a power larger than ourselves, who creates us equal, in His image, and we are confident in principles that unite and lead us onward.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句集体目标可实现；不是正在手里握着。

### 13. rise/lift sb. above sth.

**意思：** 使某人超越某种局限／利益（正式／鼓励）

> We are bound by ideals that move us beyond our backgrounds, lift us above our interests, and teach us what it means to be citizens.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 lift us above our interests；不是所有提升都用above。

### 14. at sb.'s best

**意思：** 处在最佳状态；表现最好时（中性／评价）

> America at its best matches a commitment to principle with a concern for civility.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** at its best 与正常总体评价不同。

### 15. good will and respect

**意思：** 善意与尊重（中性／人际）

> A civil society demands from each of us good will and respect, fair dealing and forgiveness.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** good will 与 goodwill 同概念；原拼法保留。

### 16. fair dealing

**意思：** 公正待人／公平交易（中性偏正式）

> A civil society demands from each of us good will and respect, fair dealing and forgiveness.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 不只买卖价格公平。

### 17. the stakes of sth.

**意思：** 某事所牵涉的得失和风险（中性／讨论）

> Some seem to believe that our politics can afford to be petty because in a time of peace the stakes of our debates appear small.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 非木桩或赌注字面；可谈争论的重要性。

### 18. If sb. does not do sth., ...

**意思：** 如果某人不做某事，就……（中性／条件警示）

> If our country does not lead the cause of freedom, it will not be led.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 本段三次表达不行动的后果，不据普通语法判已掌握。

### 19. keep a commitment

**意思：** 履行承诺（中性／工作）

> And this commitment, if we keep it, is a way to shared accomplishment.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 this commitment, if we keep it，it 回指承诺。

### 20. spare sb. from sth.

**意思：** 让某人免于某种痛苦或困难（中性）

> We will reform Social Security and Medicare, sparing our children from struggles we have the power to prevent.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 struggles；不同于给某人腾出时间。

### 21. recover the momentum of sth.

**意思：** 恢复某事的发展势头（正式／工作趋势）

> And we will reduce taxes to recover the momentum of our economy and reward the effort and enterprise of working Americans.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 非物理动量。

### 22. make no mistake

**意思：** 务必明白；不要误以为别的意思（中性／警告强调）

> The enemies of liberty and our country should make no mistake: America remains engaged in the world, by history and by choice, shaping a balance of power that favors freedom.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句主语 enemies should，语气强势。

### 23. by choice

**意思：** 出于自己的选择（中性／动机）

> The enemies of liberty and our country should make no mistake: America remains engaged in the world, by history and by choice, shaping a balance of power that favors freedom.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 与原句 by history 对照，非偶然。

### 24. bad faith

**意思：** 不诚实的意图；缺乏诚意（正式／沟通法律）

> We will meet aggression and bad faith with resolve and strength.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 与宗教信仰不同。

### 25. speak for sth.

**意思：** 为某种价值发声／代表它（中性／立场）

> And to all nations, we will speak for the values that gave our Nation birth.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是 speak for sb. 代某人决定的接法。

### 26. whatever sb.'s views of sth.

**意思：** 无论某人对某事有什么看法（正式／让步）

> And whatever our views of its cause, we can agree that children at risk are not at fault.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句省略 may be；用来先承认立场差异。

### 27. at risk

**意思：** 处于危险／容易受害的状态（中性）

> And whatever our views of its cause, we can agree that children at risk are not at fault.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 children；风险具体内容需语境。

### 28. at fault

**意思：** 有过错；应负责任（中性／判断）

> And whatever our views of its cause, we can agree that children at risk are not at fault.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句否定，强调孩子无需为贫困负责。

### 29. an act of God

**意思：** 不可抗力性质的天灾（正式／法律兼宗教）

> Abandonment and abuse are not acts of God; they are failures of love.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句反对用天灾解释遗弃和虐待；并非通常上帝的一切行为。

### 30. no substitute for sth.

**意思：** 不能代替某事（中性／界限）

> And the proliferation of prisons, however necessary, is no substitute for hope and order in our souls.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 通常说明某种手段无法弥补另一种必要条件。

### 31. however + 形容词

**意思：** 无论多么……；尽管……（论述／让步）

> And the proliferation of prisons, however necessary, is no substitute for hope and order in our souls.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 however necessary 为省略结构；仍承认必要性。

### 32. respond to sth.

**意思：** 对某事起反应／能因某事而缓解（中性）

> And some needs and hurts are so deep they will only respond to a mentor's touch or a pastor's prayer.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句人的深刻创伤会因导师关怀而缓解，非回复邮件义。

### 33. set sb. free

**意思：** 使某人获得自由／摆脱束缚（中性）

> And we find that children and community are the commitments that set us free.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句承诺使人自由为比喻，非真开牢门。

### 34. give direction to sth.

**意思：** 为某事提供方向（中性／工作）

> Our public interest depends on private character, on civic duty and family bonds and basic fairness, on uncounted, unhonored acts of decency, which give direction to our freedom.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 此处道德行为引导自由的使用。

### 35. serve the public interest

**意思：** 服务公共利益（正式／公共工作）

> I will live and lead by these principles: to advance my convictions with civility, to serve the public interest with courage, to speak for greater justice and compassion, to call for responsibility and try to live it, as well.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 非服务某个客户个人。

### 36. I ask sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 我请求某人做某事（中性／请求）

> I ask you to seek a common good beyond your comfort, to defend needed reforms against easy attacks, to serve your Nation, beginning with your neighbor.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 演讲语气直接，不代表所有工作场景礼貌程度相同。

### 37. as important as sth.

**意思：** 与某事同样重要（中性／评价）

> What you do is as important as anything Government does.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 anything Government does 是强调比较标准。

### 38. Do you not think 从句?

**意思：** 难道你不认为……吗？（正式／反问）

> Do you not think an angel rides in the whirlwind and directs this storm?''

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 历史引文引导认同；比 Don't you think 庄重，不能当无偏询问。

### 39. Much time has passed since 从句

**意思：** 自……以来已经过了很久（中性／叙事）

> Much time has passed since Jefferson arrived for his inauguration.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 保留时间起点，非所有 since 都因果。

### 40. in service to sb.

**意思：** 为某人服务；服务于某人（正式／责任）

> And our duty is fulfilled in service to one another.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P029](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book1/html/PPP-2001-book1-doc-pg1.htm)

**补充：** 原句 to one another，非 in service 设备运行状态。

## 2001-09-11 · 小布什：九一一袭击后的全国讲话

### 1. come under attack

**意思：** 受到攻击（中性偏正式／危机）

> Today our fellow citizens, our way of life, our very freedom came under attack in a series of deliberate and deadly terrorist acts.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 此处恐怖袭击，也可指观点受到批评。

### 2. a series of sth.

**意思：** 一系列某事（中性／叙述）

> Today our fellow citizens, our way of life, our very freedom came under attack in a series of deliberate and deadly terrorist acts.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 强调多次或多个相关事件。

### 3. fill sb. with sth.

**意思：** 使某人充满某种情绪（中性／情绪）

> The pictures of airplanes flying into buildings, fires burning, huge structures collapsing have filled us with disbelief, terrible sadness, and a quiet, unyielding anger.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 此处 disbelief、sadness、anger，非装满物品。

### 4. be intended to do sth.

**意思：** 意在做某事（中性／目的分析）

> These acts of mass murder were intended to frighten our Nation into chaos and retreat, but they have failed.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句袭击意图，不等于实际结果。

### 5. frighten sb. into sth./doing sth.

**意思：** 恐吓某人，使其陷入某状态／做某事（中性／胁迫）

> These acts of mass murder were intended to frighten our Nation into chaos and retreat, but they have failed.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** into 标示受恐吓导致的结果。

### 6. keep sth. from doing sth.

**意思：** 阻止某物发生某种行为（中性／限制）

> And no one will keep that light from shining.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句 light from shining 为比喻；结构不是藏住某物。

### 7. in any way sb. can

**意思：** 以某人力所能及的任何方式（中性／帮助）

> And we responded with the best of America, with the daring of our rescueworkers, with the caring for strangers and neighbors who came to give blood and help in any way they could.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句 could 随过去时间，不固定成只能 can。

### 8. give blood

**意思：** 献血（中性／生活医疗）

> And we responded with the best of America, with the daring of our rescueworkers, with the caring for strangers and neighbors who came to give blood and help in any way they could.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 非流血受伤。

### 9. emergency response plans

**意思：** 应急响应方案（正式／工作）

> Immediately following the first attack, I implemented our Government's emergency response plans.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 具体工作搭配，不收 entire 事件报道。

### 10. Our first priority is to do sth.

**意思：** 我们的首要任务是……（中性／工作说明）

> Our first priority is to get help to those who have been injured and to take every precaution to protect our citizens at home and around the world from further attacks.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 可明确事项排序，不表示别的完全不重要。

### 11. take every precaution to do sth.

**意思：** 采取一切预防措施以做某事（中性偏正式／安全）

> Our first priority is to get help to those who have been injured and to take every precaution to protect our citizens at home and around the world from further attacks.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** every 强调尽力预防，不是结果保证。

### 12. protect sb. from sth.

**意思：** 保护某人免受某种威胁（中性／边界）

> Our first priority is to get help to those who have been injured and to take every precaution to protect our citizens at home and around the world from further attacks.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** from 引出风险源。

### 13. without interruption

**意思：** 不间断地（中性／运行状态）

> The functions of our Government continue without interruption.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 可用于服务或工作持续。

### 14. open for business

**意思：** 照常营业／已恢复开展业务（中性／工作）

> Federal agencies in Washington which had to be evacuated today are reopening for essential personnel tonight and will be open for business tomorrow.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 此处政府、经济也用作恢复运作，并非全都商店。

### 15. be underway

**意思：** 正在进行中（中性／进度）

> The search is underway for those who are behind these evil acts.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句 search；不只是交通行进。

### 16. be behind sth.

**意思：** 是某事的幕后策划者／责任人（中性／归因）

> The search is underway for those who are behind these evil acts.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是空间在后面或支持计划义。

### 17. bring sb. to justice

**意思：** 将某人绳之以法（正式／法律）

> I've directed the full resources of our intelligence and law enforcement communities to find those responsible and to bring them to justice.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 要求法律追责，非私人报复。

### 18. make no distinction between A and B

**意思：** 不区分A与B，一视同仁处理（中性偏正式／原则）

> We will make no distinction between the terrorists who committed these acts and those who harbor them.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 可能是强调同等责任，不总是积极公平。

### 19. those responsible (for sth.)

**意思：** 对某事负有责任的人（中性／归因）

> I've directed the full resources of our intelligence and law enforcement communities to find those responsible and to bring them to justice.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句省略事件宾语，非通常认真负责的褒义形容。

### 20. strongly condemn sth.

**意思：** 强烈谴责某事（正式／公开立场）

> I appreciate so very much the Members of Congress who have joined me in strongly condemning these attacks.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 语气强烈，非轻度不同意。

### 21. offer sb.'s condolences

**意思：** 表达哀悼／慰问（正式／丧事）

> And on behalf of the American people, I thank the many world leaders who have called to offer their condolences and assistance.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 通常针对死亡，不用于一般工作延期。

### 22. I appreciate sth./sb. who ...

**意思：** 我感谢／欣赏某事或某人的帮助（中性／感谢）

> I appreciate so very much the Members of Congress who have joined me in strongly condemning these attacks.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 此处对国会谴责袭击表示感谢；不一定指审美欣赏。

### 23. stand together

**意思：** 团结一致（中性／支持）

> America and our friends and allies join with all those who want peace and security in the world, and we stand together to win the war against terrorism.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句反恐团结，不能把政治任务当默认槽位。

### 24. a sense of safety and security

**意思：** 安全感（中性／心理状态）

> Tonight I ask for your prayers for all those who grieve, for the children whose worlds have been shattered, for all whose sense of safety and security has been threatened.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** sense 表主观感受，不是独立安全核证。

### 25. I ask for sth.

**意思：** 我请求获得某物／支持（中性／请求）

> Tonight I ask for your prayers for all those who grieve, for the children whose worlds have been shattered, for all whose sense of safety and security has been threatened.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句 prayers 为宗教支持，工作可用于具体帮助。

### 26. from every walk of life

**意思：** 来自各行各业／社会各阶层（中性／群体）

> This is a day when all Americans from every walk of life unite in our resolve for justice and peace.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** walk of life 非走路方式。

### 27. unite in sb.'s resolve to/for sth.

**意思：** 共同坚定决心以实现某事（正式／演讲）

> This is a day when all Americans from every walk of life unite in our resolve for justice and peace.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2001-book2/html/PPP-2001-book2-doc-pg1099.htm)

**补充：** 原句 for justice and peace；to 后接动词。

## 2005-01-20 · 小布什：2005 年就职演说

### 1. be determined to do sth.

**意思：** 决心做某事（中性／决心）

> I am grateful for the honor of this hour, mindful of the consequential times in which we live, and determined to fulfill the oath that I have sworn and you have witnessed.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不是被决定安排去做。

### 2. fulfill an oath

**意思：** 履行誓言（正式／承诺）

> I am grateful for the honor of this hour, mindful of the consequential times in which we live, and determined to fulfill the oath that I have sworn and you have witnessed.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 较庄重；普通工作约定常用 promise 或 commitment。

### 3. stand watch

**意思：** 站岗；守望（正式／军事）

> For a half a century, America defended our own freedom by standing watch on distant borders.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 本句边境守卫，不声称普通日常高频。

### 4. be prone to sth.

**意思：** 容易受某事影响／倾向于某事（中性偏正式／分析）

> prone to ideologies that feed hatred and excuse murder,

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 常用于不利倾向；后接名词或 doing。

### 5. for as long as 从句

**意思：** 只要某状态还持续，就……（中性／条件持续）

> For as long as whole regions of the world simmer in resentment and tyranny, prone to ideologies that feed hatred and excuse murder, violence will gather and multiply in destructive power and cross the most defended borders and raise a mortal threat.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 兼有条件和时长，不等同任何瞬时if。

### 6. be led to one conclusion

**意思：** 由此得出一个结论（正式／论证）

> We are led, by events and common sense, to one conclusion: The survival of liberty in our land increasingly depends on the success of liberty in other lands.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句 led by events and common sense，外层推理动作。

### 7. be fit to do sth.

**意思：** 有资格／适合做某事（中性／判断）

> Across the generations, we have proclaimed the imperative of self-government, because no one is fit to be a master and no one deserves to be a slave.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 此处否定谁有资格当主宰，不是身体健康义。

### 8. with the ultimate goal of doing sth.

**意思：** 以最终做某事为目标（中性偏正式／工作）

> So it is the policy of the United States to seek and support the growth of democratic movements and institutions in every nation and culture, with the ultimate goal of ending tyranny in our world.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不等于目前步骤已达成目标。

### 9. by its nature

**意思：** 就其本质而言（中性偏正式／分析）

> Freedom, by its nature, must be chosen and defended by citizens and sustained by the rule of law and the protection of minorities.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 说明内在特性，不是天然材料。

### 10. impose sth. on sb.

**意思：** 把某事强加给某人（中性／批评）

> America will not impose our own style of government on the unwilling.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句拒绝强加政府形式。

### 11. find sb.'s own voice

**意思：** 找到属于自己的表达方式／立场（中性／成长）

> Our goal instead is to help others find their own voice, attain their own freedom, and make their own way.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不是发现声带。

### 12. make sb.'s own way

**意思：** 独立闯出自己的路（中性／成长）

> Our goal instead is to help others find their own voice, attain their own freedom, and make their own way.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 与 make one's way to 地点 的前往义区分。

### 13. sth. is no excuse for doing sth.

**意思：** 某事不能成为做另一件事的借口（中性／责任）

> The difficulty of the task is no excuse for avoiding it.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句难度不是逃避理由，语气可能责备。

### 14. sth. is not unlimited, but sth. is considerable

**意思：** 某事并非无限，但仍相当可观（正式／界定范围）

> America's influence is not unlimited, but fortunately for the oppressed, America's influence is considerable and we will use it confidently in freedom's cause.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句承认影响力局限再肯定实际作用。

### 15. test sb.'s resolve

**意思：** 考验某人的决心（中性偏正式／对抗）

> Some have unwisely chosen to test America's resolve and have found it firm.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 此处威胁是否坚定，不是笔试。

### 16. at the mercy of sb./sth.

**意思：** 任凭某人／事摆布；受制于……（中性／困境）

> America will not pretend that jailed dissidents prefer their chains or that women welcome humiliation and servitude or that any human being aspires to live at the mercy of bullies.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不是得到对方仁慈照顾。

### 17. make clear that 从句

**意思：** 明确说明……（中性／工作沟通）

> We will encourage reform in other governments by making clear that success in our relations will require the decent treatment of their own people.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句 making clear that，保留从句内容。

### 18. In the long run

**意思：** 从长期来看（中性／评价规划）

> In the long run, there is no justice without freedom and there can be no human rights without human liberty.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不指实际长跑。

### 19. will not pretend that 从句

**意思：** 不会假装……是真的（中性／立场）

> America will not pretend that jailed dissidents prefer their chains or that women welcome humiliation and servitude or that any human being aspires to live at the mercy of bullies.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 否定一个被指为虚假的主张，语气坚定。

### 20. sb., of all people

**意思：** 偏偏某人；尤其是某人（中性／惊讶强调）

> Americans, of all people, should never be surprised by the power of our ideals.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句 Americans, of all people 强调不应该意外的人。

### 21. stand for sth.

**意思：** 坚持／捍卫某种价值（中性／立场）

> When you stand for your liberty, we will stand with you.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 此处非缩写代表或参选义。

### 22. stand with sb.

**意思：** 与某人站在一起／支持某人（中性／支持）

> When you stand for your liberty, we will stand with you.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 常用于有挑战的集体立场，亦可字面并肩。

### 23. see sb. for who sb. is

**意思：** 看清并认可某人的真实身份／价值（中性／评价）

> Democratic reformers facing repression, prison, or exile can know: America sees you for who you are, the future leaders of your free country.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句看到受压制改革者作为未来领导人的身份。

### 24. deny sth. to sb.

**意思：** 不给某人某种权利或资源（正式／权利）

> The rulers of outlaw regimes can know that we still believe as Abraham Lincoln did: ``Those who deny freedom to others deserve it not for themselves and, under the rule of a just God, cannot long retain it.''

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原历史引文 deny freedom to others，非否认事实。

### 25. walk at sb.'s side

**意思：** 陪伴并支持某人前行（正式／演讲比喻）

> Start on this journey of progress and justice, and America will walk at your side.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句外交进步历程，不将整个旅程固定化。

### 26. rely on sb.'s counsel

**意思：** 依赖某人的建议（正式／协作）

> And all the allies of the United States can know: We honor your friendship; we rely on your counsel; and we depend on your help.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** counsel 这里建议，不是律师身份。

### 27. concerted effort

**意思：** 协调一致的共同努力（中性偏正式／工作）

> The concerted effort of free nations to promote democracy is a prelude to our enemies' defeat.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** concerted 非音乐会。

### 28. a prelude to sth.

**意思：** 某事的前奏／预兆（正式／分析）

> The concerted effort of free nations to promote democracy is a prelude to our enemies' defeat.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 表示后续事件之前的阶段，不断言必然因果。

### 29. in good measure

**意思：** 充分地；相当多地（正式／评价）

> From all of you I have asked patience in the hard task of securing America, which you have granted in good measure.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句指给了充分耐心；不同于 for good measure 的额外补充义。

### 30. accept obligations

**意思：** 承担义务（正式／责任）

> Our country has accepted obligations that are difficult to fulfill and would be dishonorable to abandon.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 非只是认可别人有义务。

### 31. be difficult to fulfill

**意思：** 难以履行（中性偏正式／责任）

> Our country has accepted obligations that are difficult to fulfill and would be dishonorable to abandon.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句义务，可用于承诺；不同于难以满足愿望。

### 32. show sb.'s devotion to sth.

**意思：** 表现出对某事的忠诚／奉献（正式／评价）

> Some have shown their devotion to our country in deaths that honored their whole lives, and we will always honor their names and their sacrifice.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句死亡体现献身，避免移植整句夸张到工作。

### 33. for the first time

**意思：** 第一次（中性／叙事）

> All Americans have witnessed this idealism and some for the first time.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 保留经历时间限定，不据简单判掌握。

### 34. a cause larger than oneself

**意思：** 超越个人利益的事业（正式／演讲）

> Make the choice to serve in a cause larger than your wants, larger than yourself, and in your days you will add not just to the wealth of our country but to its character.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句 larger than your wants, larger than yourself；非万能日常模板。

### 35. have need of sth.

**意思：** 需要某事（正式／论述）

> America has need of idealism and courage because we have essential work at home, the unfinished work of American freedom.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 比 need sth. 庄重，原句 idealism and courage。

### 36. serve the needs of sth.

**意思：** 满足某事／某时期的需要（中性偏正式／工作）

> And now we will extend this vision by reforming great institutions to serve the needs of our time.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不一定商业服务。

### 37. in the end

**意思：** 归根结底；最终（中性／总结）

> Self-government relies, in the end, on the governing of the self.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 此处强调自治最终依靠自律，不只故事时间结尾。

### 38. look after sb.

**意思：** 照顾某人（中性／生活）

> Our Nation relies on men and women who look after a neighbor and surround the lost with love.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句邻里互助，非寻找。

### 39. at the same time

**意思：** 同时（中性／约束）

> And our country must abandon all the habits of racism, because we cannot carry the message of freedom and the baggage of bigotry at the same time.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句说不能同时传递自由又背负偏见，说明相容性。

### 40. from the perspective of sth.

**意思：** 从某种角度来看（中性偏正式／分析）

> From the perspective of a single day, including this day of dedication, the issues and questions before our country are many.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句一天与世纪时间尺度对比。

### 41. bring credit to sth.

**意思：** 为某事增光／赢得好评价（正式／评价）

> And did our character bring credit to that cause?

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** credit 非贷款；此处品格使事业受到尊重。

### 42. in good faith

**意思：** 真诚地；怀着诚意（中性偏正式／承诺）

> We have known divisions, which must be healed to move forward in great purposes, and I will strive in good faith to heal them.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 不等于宗教虔诚信仰。

### 43. an ebb and flow of sth.

**意思：** 某事的起落消长（中性偏正式／趋势）

> History has an ebb and flow of justice, but history also has a visible direction, set by liberty and the Author of Liberty.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句 justice；不是海潮字面。

### 44. wave upon wave of sth.

**意思：** 一波又一波某物／人（叙事／演讲）

> When our Founders declared a new order of the ages, when soldiers died in wave upon wave for a union based on liberty, when citizens marched in peaceful outrage under the banner ``Freedom Now,'' they were acting on an ancient hope that is meant to be fulfilled.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句士兵接连牺牲，有连续涌现意味。

### 45. be meant to do sth.

**意思：** 被寄望／本应／旨在做某事（中性／目的）

> When our Founders declared a new order of the ages, when soldiers died in wave upon wave for a union based on liberty, when citizens marched in peaceful outrage under the banner ``Freedom Now,'' they were acting on an ancient hope that is meant to be fulfilled.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句说一种希望理应得到实现，带使命感；不是事实已经兑现。

### 46. as if 从句

**意思：** 仿佛……一样（中性／描述）

> When the Declaration of Independence was first read in public and the Liberty Bell was sounded in celebration, a witness said, ``It rang as if it meant something.'' In our time, it means something still.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句历史引文 It rang as if it meant something，不把引述内容当作者事实。

### 47. watch over sb./sth.

**意思：** 照看／守护某人或某物（中性）

> May God bless you, and may He watch over the United States of America.

**来源：** [George W. Bush · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/PPP-2005-book1/html/PPP-2005-book1-doc-pg66.htm)

**补充：** 原句宗教祝福，普通照看义可迁移但不改原场景。

## 2009-01-20 · 奥巴马：2009 年就职演说

### 1. humbled by sth.

**意思：** 因某种责任或经历而感到谦卑（正式／感谢）

> My fellow citizens, I stand here today humbled by the task before us, grateful for the trust you have bestowed, mindful of the sacrifices borne by our ancestors.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不是受羞辱；常用于承接重任。

### 2. mindful of sth.

**意思：** 牢记并顾及某事（正式／工作论述）

> My fellow citizens, I stand here today humbled by the task before us, grateful for the trust you have bestowed, mindful of the sacrifices borne by our ancestors.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 后接名词，表示有意识地考虑。

### 3. thank sb. for sth.

**意思：** 为某事向某人致谢（中性／感谢）

> I thank President Bush for his service to our Nation, as well as the generosity and cooperation he has shown throughout this transition.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 保留具体感谢理由的表达动作；不据简单判定已掌握。

### 4. every so often

**意思：** 偶尔；隔一阵子（中性／叙事）

> Yet every so often, the oath is taken amidst gathering clouds and raging storms.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 非规律地发生，不等于 every day。

### 5. carry on

**意思：** 继续坚持做下去（中性）

> At these moments, America has carried on not simply because of the skill or vision of those in high office, but because we the people have remained faithful to the ideals of our forbearers and true to our founding documents.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句无宾语；可另接 doing sth.。

### 6. remain faithful to sth.

**意思：** 始终忠于某种理想或承诺（正式／立场）

> At these moments, America has carried on not simply because of the skill or vision of those in high office, but because we the people have remained faithful to the ideals of our forbearers and true to our founding documents.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 适合价值或承诺，不是每种维持状态。

### 7. in the midst of sth.

**意思：** 正处于某事之中（中性偏正式）

> That we are in the midst of crisis is now well understood.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 常用来说明危机、活动或变化的阶段。

### 8. on the part of sb.

**意思：** 某人方面的；由某人表现出的（正式／归因）

> Our economy is badly weakened, a consequence of greed and irresponsibility on the part of some, but also our collective failure to make hard choices and prepare the Nation for a new age.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不是某物的一部分。

### 9. make hard choices

**意思：** 作出艰难取舍（中性／工作）

> Our economy is badly weakened, a consequence of greed and irresponsibility on the part of some, but also our collective failure to make hard choices and prepare the Nation for a new age.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** hard 指难以取舍，非难理解。

### 10. no less + 形容词

**意思：** 同样地；丝毫不逊色（论述）
