> Less measurable but no less profound is a sapping of confidence across our land, a nagging fear that America's decline is inevitable, that the next generation must lower its sights.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 less measurable but no less profound 对比可测性与影响。

### 11. a nagging fear that 从句

**意思：** 挥之不去的担忧：……（中性／心理叙述）

> Less measurable but no less profound is a sapping of confidence across our land, a nagging fear that America's decline is inevitable, that the next generation must lower its sights.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** nagging 指反复缠绕，不是别人唠叨。

### 12. lower sb.'s sights

**意思：** 降低自己的目标或期望（中性／评价）

> Less measurable but no less profound is a sapping of confidence across our land, a nagging fear that America's decline is inevitable, that the next generation must lower its sights.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** sights 不是此处的视力。

### 13. in a short span of time

**意思：** 在很短的时间内（中性偏正式）

> They will not be met easily or in a short span of time.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** span 指一段持续时间。

### 14. choose sth. over sth.

**意思：** 在两者间选择前者（中性／选择）

> On this day, we gather because we have chosen hope over fear, unity of purpose over conflict and discord.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** over 表偏好，不是空间上方。

### 15. for far too long

**意思：** 已经持续太久（中性／批评）

> On this day, we come to proclaim an end to the petty grievances and false promises, the recriminations and worn-out dogmas that for far too long have strangled our politics.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** far 强化 too long；暗示应当改变。

### 16. The time has come to do sth.

**意思：** 到了该做某事的时候（正式／宣布行动）

> We remain a young nation, but in the words of Scripture, the time has come to set aside childish things.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 语气比 it's time 更庄重。

### 17. set aside sth.

**意思：** 放下；暂不考虑某事（中性）

> We remain a young nation, but in the words of Scripture, the time has come to set aside childish things.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处为放下幼稚行为，非预留资金义。

### 18. carry forward sth.

**意思：** 继续推进或传承某事（正式／工作）

> The time has come to reaffirm our enduring spirit, to choose our better history, to carry forward that precious gift, that noble idea passed on from generation to generation: the God-given promise that all are equal, all are free, and all deserve a chance to pursue their full measure of happiness.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 可接工作、计划或传统；不指一般携带。

### 19. pass sth. on (to sb.)

**意思：** 把某物传给他人（中性）

> The time has come to reaffirm our enduring spirit, to choose our better history, to carry forward that precious gift, that noble idea passed on from generation to generation: the God-given promise that all are equal, all are free, and all deserve a chance to pursue their full measure of happiness.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句被动 passed on；对象为理念。

### 20. [X] is never a given

**意思：** X 从来不是理所当然或自动有保证的（中性／论述）

> greatness is never a given.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** a given 作名词指可当成既定事实的东西；不能理解为过去分词被给予。

> Trust is never a given; you have to earn it.（教学自造）

### 21. settle for less

**意思：** 接受更低的标准或次一等的结果；将就（日常／鼓励或评价）

> Our journey has never been one of shortcuts or settling for less.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** settle for 指接受不那么理想的选项；less 的比较标准由语境给出。

> We don't have to settle for less just because we're in a hurry.（教学自造）

### 22. in search of sth.

**意思：** 寻找某物或某种生活（中性／叙事）

> For us, they packed up their few worldly possessions and traveled across oceans in search of a new life.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 介词短语，可修饰旅行或行动。

### 23. pack up sth.

**意思：** 收拾打包某物（中性／生活）

> For us, they packed up their few worldly possessions and traveled across oceans in search of a new life.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句是打包随身物品；不混入停工义。

### 24. time and again

**意思：** 一再；屡次（中性／叙事）

> Time and again, these men and women struggled and sacrificed and worked ‘til their hands were raw so that we might live a better life.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 强调整段经历里的重复。

### 25. the sum of sth.

**意思：** 某些部分的总和（中性／论述）

> They saw America as bigger than the sum of our individual ambitions, greater than all the differences of birth or wealth or faction.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句用 bigger than the sum 对比整体与部分；不将政治比喻整句固定化。

### 26. stand pat

**意思：** 维持原样，不作改变（中性／评价）

> But our time of standing pat, of protecting narrow interests and putting off unpleasant decisions, that time has surely passed.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 standing pat；带保守不动的意味。

### 27. put off sth.

**意思：** 推迟某事（中性／工作生活）

> But our time of standing pat, of protecting narrow interests and putting off unpleasant decisions, that time has surely passed.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处接 decisions；不是使人反感义。

### 28. pick oneself up

**意思：** 重新振作起来（中性／鼓励）

> Starting today, we must pick ourselves up, dust ourselves off, and begin again the work of remaking America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处为受挫后的恢复，亦有字面起身义。

### 29. dust oneself off

**意思：** 受挫后整理心情重新开始（中性／鼓励）

> Starting today, we must pick ourselves up, dust ourselves off, and begin again the work of remaking America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 与 pick oneself up 相接；此处为比喻。

### 30. call for sth.

**意思：** 需要；要求某事（中性／判断）

> The state of the economy calls for action, bold and swift, and we will act not only to create new jobs but to lay a new foundation for growth.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处是形势需要行动，不是呼叫某人。

### 31. lay a foundation for sth.

**意思：** 为某事奠定基础（中性／工作）

> The state of the economy calls for action, bold and swift, and we will act not only to create new jobs but to lay a new foundation for growth.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** foundation 可比喻后续发展条件。

### 32. meet the demands of sth.

**意思：** 满足某事提出的要求（中性／工作）

> And we will transform our schools and colleges and universities to meet the demands of a new age.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** demands 为要求，非 demand 动词。

### 33. There are some who 从句

**意思：** 有些人认为／做……（论述）

> Now, there are some who question the scale of our ambitions, who suggest that our system cannot tolerate too many big plans.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 引入一种立场，原句 question 与 suggest 说明具体异议。

### 34. question the scale of sth.

**意思：** 质疑某事的规模（正式／讨论）

> Now, there are some who question the scale of our ambitions, who suggest that our system cannot tolerate too many big plans.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** question 作动词；不等同询问尺寸。

### 35. The question is not whether [A], but whether [B]

**意思：** 真正要问的不是 A，而是 B；重新界定讨论焦点（工作讨论／论述）

> The question we ask today is not whether our Government is too big or too small, but whether it works;

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** whether A/B 可各接一个完整判断；不只是替换 but，而是在转移判定标准。

> The question is not whether we can build it, but whether anyone needs it.（教学自造）

### 36. What sb. fails to understand is that 从句

**意思：** 某人没有理解的是……（论述／较直接）

> What the cynics fail to understand is that the ground has shifted beneath them; that the stale political arguments that have consumed us for so long no longer apply.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 指明对方遗漏，工作中可能显得强硬。

### 37. hold sb. to account

**意思：** 要求某人负责并接受问责（正式／责任）

> And those of us who manage the public's dollars will be held to account to spend wisely, reform bad habits, and do our business in the light of day, because only then can we restore the vital trust between a people and their government.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句被动 be held to account；不等于记账。

### 38. in the light of day

**意思：** 公开地；在公众可见之处（中性偏正式）

> And those of us who manage the public's dollars will be held to account to spend wisely, reform bad habits, and do our business in the light of day, because only then can we restore the vital trust between a people and their government.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句指政府透明操作，也可字面指白天。

### 39. Where the answer is yes/no, ...

**意思：** 如果答案是肯定／否定的，就……（正式／条件论证）

> Where the answer is yes, we intend to move forward.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** where 此处不是物理地点，而是适用情形。

### 40. not out of [X], but because [Y]

**意思：** 不是出于 X，而是因为 Y；澄清动机（口语书面均可／解释）

> not out of charity, but because it is the surest route to our common good.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** out of 在此引出动机，X 常为情绪或态度名词，Y 为从句；不要照抄原政治主张。

> I asked not out of curiosity, but because I needed to make a decision.（教学自造）

### 41. spin out of control

**意思：** 迅速失去控制（中性／问题描述）

> But this crisis has reminded us that without a watchful eye, the market can spin out of control.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** spin 带失控发展的意味。

### 42. As for sth.

**意思：** 至于某事；转到另一个话题（中性／衔接）

> As for our common defense, we reject as false the choice between our safety and our ideals.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 引出已相关的议题，不必机械接每段。

### 43. for sth.'s sake

**意思：** 为了某事；看在某事份上（中性／动机）

> Those ideals still light the world, and we will not give them up for expedience's sake.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 expedience's sake 为图方便，语境正式。

### 44. face down sth.

**意思：** 坚决对抗并压制某种威胁（中性偏正式）

> Recall that earlier generations faced down fascism and communism not just with missiles and tanks but with sturdy alliances and enduring convictions.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 比 face 更有不退缩并迫使对方让步的意味。

### 45. entitle sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 使某人有权做某事（正式／权利边界）

> They understood that our power alone cannot protect us, nor does it entitle us to do as we please.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 否定可用于澄清资格并不授权任意行动。

### 46. do as one pleases

**意思：** 随心所欲地做（中性／批评）

> They understood that our power alone cannot protect us, nor does it entitle us to do as we please.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 do as we please；可能带未经约束的负面意思。

### 47. work tirelessly to do sth.

**意思：** 不懈努力做某事（正式／承诺）

> With old friends and former foes, we will work tirelessly to lessen the nuclear threat and roll back the specter of a warming planet.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** tirelessly 强调持续投入，日常承诺不宜夸大。

### 48. roll back sth.

**意思：** 逆转；削弱某种发展或影响（正式／政策工作）

> With old friends and former foes, we will work tirelessly to lessen the nuclear threat and roll back the specter of a warming planet.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 与 rollback 软件含义相关但此处为遏制趋势。

### 49. cannot help but do sth.

**意思：** 不由得做某事；忍不住……（中性）

> we cannot help but believe that the old hatreds shall someday pass;

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处是相信；but 后动词原形。

### 50. usher in a new era of sth.

**意思：** 开启某事的新时期（正式／演讲）

> America must play its role in ushering in a new era of peace.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 庄重宣布改变，不声称日常高频。

### 51. blame sth. on sb.

**意思：** 把某个问题归咎于某人（中性／归因）

> To those leaders around the globe who seek to sow conflict or blame their society's ills on the West, know that your people will judge you on what you can build, not what you destroy.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 被指责的事在 blame 后，责任对象在 on 后。

### 52. cling to sth.

**意思：** 紧抓住某事不放（中性／评价）

> To those who cling to power through corruption and deceit and the silencing of dissent, know that you are on the wrong side of history, but that we will extend a hand if you are willing to unclench your fist.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处 cling to power；不只字面抓住。

### 53. extend a hand

**意思：** 伸出援手；表示愿意合作（正式／和解）

> To those who cling to power through corruption and deceit and the silencing of dissent, know that you are on the wrong side of history, but that we will extend a hand if you are willing to unclench your fist.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句与 unclench your fist 构成修辞，只收可迁移部分。

### 54. can no longer afford sth.

**意思：** 再也承受不起某种行为或状态（中性／立场）

> And to those nations like ours that enjoy relative plenty, we say we can no longer afford indifference to suffering outside our borders, nor can we consume the world's resources without regard to effect, for the world has changed, and we must change with it.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是金钱购买；表达继续如此代价太高。

### 55. without regard to sth.

**意思：** 不顾及某事（正式／批评）

> And to those nations like ours that enjoy relative plenty, we say we can no longer afford indifference to suffering outside our borders, nor can we consume the world's resources without regard to effect, for the world has changed, and we must change with it.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 后接后果、差异等名词。

### 56. find meaning in sth.

**意思：** 从某事中找到意义（中性／价值）

> We honor them not only because they are guardians of our liberty, but because they embody the spirit of service, a willingness to find meaning in something greater than themselves.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不是理解一句话的含义。

### 57. see sb. through [a difficult period]

**意思：** 帮助某人撑过困难时期（中性）

> which sees us through our darkest hours.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不是看穿某人；与 see through an excuse（识破借口）区分。原句包含 which 回指，不能把截取片段当完整句。

> Her support saw me through a very difficult year.（教学自造）

### 58. take in a stranger

**意思：** 收留陌生人（中性／生活）

> It is the kindness to take in a stranger when the levees break, the selflessness of workers who would rather cut their hours than see a friend lose their job, which sees us through our darkest hours.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** take in 的收留义；与欺骗、领会义区分。

### 59. would rather do sth. than do sth.

**意思：** 宁愿做前者也不愿后者（中性／取舍）

> It is the kindness to take in a stranger when the levees break, the selflessness of workers who would rather cut their hours than see a friend lose their job, which sees us through our darkest hours.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句宁减工时不愿朋友失业。

### 60. What is required of sb. is sth.

**意思：** 对某人的要求是……（正式／解释）

> What is required of us now is a new era of responsibility, a recognition on the part of every American that we have duties to ourselves, our Nation, and the world.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 now is；把责任明确给对象。

### 61. firm in the knowledge that 从句

**意思：** 坚定地知道／确信……（正式／立场）

> Duties that we do not grudgingly accept but, rather, seize gladly, firm in the knowledge that there is nothing so satisfying to the spirit, so defining of our character, than giving our all to a difficult task.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不声称日常口语套语。

### 62. give sb.'s all to sth.

**意思：** 全力投入某事（中性／决心）

> Duties that we do not grudgingly accept but, rather, seize gladly, firm in the knowledge that there is nothing so satisfying to the spirit, so defining of our character, than giving our all to a difficult task.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** all 指全部努力，不是全部财物。

### 63. call on sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 号召／要求某人做某事（正式／行动）

> This is the source of our confidence—the knowledge that God calls on us to shape an uncertain destiny.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 与 call for sth. 接法和对象不同。

### 64. join in sth.

**意思：** 参加某项共同活动（中性／生活）

> This is the meaning of our liberty and our creed; why men and women and children of every race and every faith can join in celebration across this magnificent Mall, and why a man whose father less than 60 years ago might not have been served at a local restaurant can now stand before you to take a most sacred oath.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 celebration；不同于 join 某个组织。

### 65. how far sb. has come/traveled

**意思：** 某人已经走了多远；取得多少进展（中性／回顾）

> So let us mark this day with remembrance of who we are and how far we have traveled.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 traveled 有旅程意象，非固定政治口号。

### 66. in doubt

**意思：** 尚无定论；不确定（中性）

> At a moment when the outcome of our Revolution was most in doubt, the Father of our Nation ordered these words be read to the people:

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句 outcome ... most in doubt 表示结果最悬而未决。

### 67. nothing but sth.

**意思：** 只有某事；除了某事别无其他（中性／限定）

> "Let it be told to the future world . . . that in the depth of winter, when nothing but hope and virtue could survive . . . that the city and the country, alarmed at one common danger, came forth to meet [it]."

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 原句为历史引文，nothing but 严格保留限制范围。

### 68. in the face of sth.

**意思：** 面对某种困难或危险（中性偏正式）

> America, in the face of our common dangers, in this winter of our hardship, let us remember these timeless words.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 不是看着某人脸的字面义。

### 69. refuse to do sth.

**意思：** 拒绝做某事（中性／决心）

> Let it be said by our children's children that when we were tested, we refused to let this journey end; that we did not turn back, nor did we falter.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-200900001/html/DCPD-200900001.htm)

**补充：** 可明确表达不愿让某结果发生，未推定用户已掌握。

## 2013-01-21 · 奥巴马：2013 年就职演说

### 1. bear witness to sth.

**意思：** 见证某事（正式／演讲）

> Each time we gather to inaugurate a President we bear witness to the enduring strength of our Constitution.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 用于说明一种事实的存在，语气庄重。

### 2. What makes sb./sth. + 形容词 is sth.

**意思：** 使某人／某物具有某种特点的是……（中性／解释）

> What makes us exceptional—what makes us American—is our allegiance to an idea articulated in a declaration made more than two centuries ago:

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句两次 what makes 重复为演讲强调，骨架可迁移。

### 3. replace sth. with sth.

**意思：** 用后者替换前者（中性／工作）

> The patriots of 1776 did not fight to replace the tyranny of a king with the privileges of a few or the rule of a mob.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 保留替换方向，不因基本词而删除。

### 4. vow to do sth.

**意思：** 郑重承诺做某事（正式／决心）

> We made ourselves anew, and vowed to move forward together.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 比普通 promise 庄重。

### 5. determine that 从句

**意思：** 确定／判定……（正式／判断）

> Together, we determined that a modern economy requires railroads and highways to speed travel and commerce, schools and colleges to train our workers.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句集体确定社会需要，非 determine to 决心接法。

### 6. fair play

**意思：** 公平竞争；按规则办事（中性／评价）

> Together, we discovered that a free market only thrives when there are rules to ensure competition and fair play.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不限体育场景。

### 7. care for sb.

**意思：** 照顾某人（中性／生活）

> Together, we resolved that a great nation must care for the vulnerable and protect its people from life's worst hazards and misfortune.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句照顾弱势者，不是喜欢某人的义项。

### 8. Through it all

**意思：** 在经历这一切的过程中始终（中性／回顾）

> Through it all, we have never relinquished our skepticism of central authority nor have we succumbed to the fiction that all society's ills can be cured through government alone.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 承接此前讲述的困难或变化。

### 9. succumb to sth.

**意思：** 屈服于；受到某事支配（正式／评价）

> Through it all, we have never relinquished our skepticism of central authority nor have we succumbed to the fiction that all society's ills can be cured through government alone.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 此处是接受一种虚构观点，不单列 succumb 义项。

### 10. insistence on sth.

**意思：** 对某事的坚持或要求（中性偏正式）

> Our celebration of initiative and enterprise, our insistence on hard work and personal responsibility, these are constants in our character.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** on 后名词或 doing，不是 insist to。

### 11. when sth. changes, so must sb./sth.

**意思：** 某事变化，某人／物也必须变化（论述）

> But we have always understood that when times change, so must we; that fidelity to our founding principles requires new responses to new challenges; that preserving our individual freedoms ultimately requires collective action.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** so 引倒装表示同样，原句 times change, so must we。

### 12. can no more do A than do B

**意思：** A 与 B 一样都做不到（正式／比较论证）

> For the American people can no more meet the demands of today's world by acting alone than American soldiers could have met the forces of fascism or communism with muskets and militias.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不同于 no more than 的数量限制；须完整看两端。

### 13. Now more than ever

**意思：** 现在比以往任何时候更……（中性／强调）

> Now more than ever, we must do these things together, as one nation and one people.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 接当前尤其迫切的需求。

### 14. steel sb.'s resolve

**意思：** 使决心更加坚定（正式／演讲）

> This generation of Americans has been tested by crises that steeled our resolve and proved our resilience.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** steel 为动词，不是钢材名词。

### 15. have a gift for sth.

**意思：** 在某事上有天赋（中性／评价）

> America's possibilities are limitless, for we possess all the qualities that this world without boundaries demands: youth and drive; diversity and openness; an endless capacity for risk and a gift for reinvention.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** gift 非此处礼物；原句 a gift for reinvention。

### 16. so long as 从句

**意思：** 只要……（中性／条件）

> My fellow Americans, we are made for this moment and we will seize it—so long as we seize it together.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句把团结设为成功条件，不指时长。

### 17. barely make it

**意思：** 勉强撑下去／过活（中性／生活）

> For we, the people, understand that our country cannot succeed when a shrinking few do very well and a growing many barely make it.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 此处经济处境，非赶上车或考试通过义。

### 18. on/from the brink of sth.

**意思：** 濒临／摆脱某种险境边缘（中性偏正式）

> We know that America thrives when every person can find independence and pride in their work; when the wages of honest labor liberate families from the brink of hardship.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句 from the brink of hardship 表示脱离困境边缘。

### 19. have the same chance to do sth. as sb.

**意思：** 有与某人同样的机会做某事（中性／公平）

> We are true to our creed when a little girl born into the bleakest poverty knows that she has the same chance to succeed as anybody else, because she is an American; she is free and she is equal, not just in the eyes of God, but also in our own.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** same 后保留 as 对照对象。

### 20. in the eyes of sb.

**意思：** 在某人看来；按某人的评价（中性）

> We are true to our creed when a little girl born into the bleakest poverty knows that she has the same chance to succeed as anybody else, because she is an American; she is free and she is equal, not just in the eyes of God, but also in our own.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 此处分别说 God 与我们，不是视力。

### 21. inadequate to the needs of sth.

**意思：** 不足以满足某事的需要（正式／评价）

> We understand that outworn programs are inadequate to the needs of our time.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 可用于说明旧办法无法应对新要求。

### 22. have nowhere to turn

**意思：** 无人可求助；无处寻求支持（中性／困境）

> For we remember the lessons of our past, when twilight years were spent in poverty and parents of a child with a disability had nowhere to turn.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** turn 此处是求助，不是转弯。

### 23. choose between doing A and doing B

**意思：** 在两种做法之间作选择（中性／取舍）

> But we reject the belief that America must choose between caring for the generation that built this country and investing in the generation that will build its future.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句 reject belief 反对被迫二选一。

### 24. no matter how + 形容词/副词 + 从句

**意思：** 无论多么……（中性／让步）

> We recognize that no matter how responsibly we live our lives, any one of us at any time may face a job loss or a sudden illness or a home swept away in a terrible storm.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 此处 responsibly，主句仍可能遭遇不幸。

### 25. be reserved for sb.

**意思：** 仅留给某些人；专供某人（中性／界限）

> We do not believe that in this country freedom is reserved for the lucky, or happiness for the few.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句否定自由只属于幸运者。

### 26. free sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 使某人能够放开手去做某事（中性）

> They do not make us a nation of takers; they free us to take the risks that make this country great.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不是本句收费义；解除限制、创造条件。

### 27. failure to do sth.

**意思：** 未能做某事（中性偏正式）

> We will respond to the threat of climate change, knowing that the failure to do so would betray our children and future generations.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 名词结构可作主语，适合分析后果。

### 28. lend meaning to sth.

**意思：** 赋予某事意义（正式／评价）

> That's what will lend meaning to the creed our fathers once declared.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** lend 在此不是借钱；不将 creed 政治词写进泛化形式。

### 29. know too well sth.

**意思：** 对某事深有体会（中性／经历）

> Our citizens, seared by the memory of those we have lost, know too well the price that is paid for liberty.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** too well 强调常因痛苦经历而熟知。

### 30. do sb. harm

**意思：** 伤害某人（中性）

> The knowledge of their sacrifice will keep us forever vigilant against those who would do us harm.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** harm 作名词，区别 do harm to sth. 接法。

### 31. resolve sb.'s differences

**意思：** 解决彼此的分歧（中性／沟通工作）

> We will show the courage to try and resolve our differences with other nations peacefully—not because we are naive about the dangers we face, but because engagement can more durably lift suspicion and fear.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不是抹掉所有差别；通常通过协商。

### 32. not because 从句, but because 从句

**意思：** 不是因为……，而是因为……（中性／澄清理由）

> We will show the courage to try and resolve our differences with other nations peacefully—not because we are naive about the dangers we face, but because engagement can more durably lift suspicion and fear.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 与 not out of 名词 的接法有独立差异，不据课本出现排除。

### 33. have a stake in sth.

**意思：** 某事关系到自身利益；有利害关系（中性／讨论）

> And we will renew those institutions that extend our capacity to manage crisis abroad, for no one has a greater stake in a peaceful world than its most powerful nation.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 此处世界和平关系到国家利益；非仅指股权。

### 34. on behalf of sb.

**意思：** 代表某人；为某人的利益（中性／工作）

> We will support democracy from Asia to Africa, from the Americas to the Middle East, because our interests and our conscience compel us to act on behalf of those who long for freedom.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句为自由渴望者发声，不只代理签字。

### 35. be inextricably bound to sth.

**意思：** 与某事不可分割地联系在一起（正式／论述）

> our individual freedom is inextricably bound to the freedom of every soul on Earth.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 语域比 be linked to 更庄重。

### 36. sth. is not complete until 从句

**意思：** 某事要到……才算完成（中性／目标边界）

> For our journey is not complete until our wives, our mothers and daughters can earn a living equal to their efforts.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原段多次重复；可迁移到项目验收条件。

### 37. earn a living

**意思：** 谋生（中性／生活）

> For our journey is not complete until our wives, our mothers and daughters can earn a living equal to their efforts.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不是单纯赚一笔钱。

### 38. exercise the right to do sth.

**意思：** 行使做某事的权利（正式／权利）

> Our journey is not complete until no citizen is forced to wait for hours to exercise the right to vote.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** exercise 非锻炼义。

### 39. safe from harm

**意思：** 免受伤害；安全无虞（中性）

> Our journey is not complete until all our children, from the streets of Detroit to the hills of Appalachia, to the quiet lanes of Newtown, know that they are cared for and cherished and always safe from harm.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** from 接威胁来源，此处概括 harm。

### 40. doing sth. does not require sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 做一件事并不要求某人做另一件事（中性／澄清界限）

> Being true to our founding documents does not require us to agree on every contour of life.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 避免把必要条件强加给对方。

### 41. It does not mean 从句

**意思：** 这并不意味着……（中性／澄清）

> It does not mean we all define liberty in exactly the same way or follow the same precise path to happiness.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** it 回指前述主张；保留外层否定推论动作。

### 42. for all time

**意思：** 永远；永久地（正式／时间范围）

> Progress does not compel us to settle centuries-long debates about the role of government for all time, but it does require us to act in our time.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句非要求一次解决永久争论。

### 43. cannot afford delay

**意思：** 不能再拖延（中性／工作决心）

> For now decisions are upon us and we cannot afford delay.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** afford 指代价承受，不指支付延期费。

### 44. mistake sth. for sth.

**意思：** 把前者误认为后者（中性／辨析）

> We cannot mistake absolutism for principle or substitute spectacle for politics or treat name-calling as reasoned debate.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不要颠倒对象。

### 45. substitute sth. for sth.

**意思：** 以前者替代后者（中性／工作）

> We cannot mistake absolutism for principle or substitute spectacle for politics or treat name-calling as reasoned debate.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 与 replace A with B 的宾语方向不同。

### 46. treat sth. as sth.

**意思：** 把某事当作另一种事来对待（中性／判断）

> We cannot mistake absolutism for principle or substitute spectacle for politics or treat name-calling as reasoned debate.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句批评把辱骂当成理性讨论。

### 47. It will be up to sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 将由某人负责做某事（中性／责任）

> We must act, we must act knowing that today's victories will be only partial and that it will be up to those who stand here in 4 years and 40 years and 400 years hence to advance the timeless spirit once conferred to us in a spare Philadelphia hall.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不是某人能否做到的能力判断。

### 48. act knowing that 从句

**意思：** 在知道／清楚……的情况下行动（中性偏正式／立场）

> We must act, knowing that our work will be imperfect.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** knowing本身不自动等于明知却仍；原句承认行动的成果可能不完美。

### 49. not so different from sth.

**意思：** 与某事并没有那么不同（中性／比较）

> But the words I spoke today are not so different from the oath that is taken each time a soldier signs up for duty or an immigrant realizes her dream.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** so 限定差异程度，不完全等同。

### 50. fill sb.'s heart with pride

**意思：** 使某人心中充满自豪（中性偏演讲／情绪）

> My oath is not so different from the pledge we all make to the flag that waves above and that fills our hearts with pride.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 普通交流可理解，但此场景庄重。

### 51. in defense of sth.

**意思：** 为维护某事；为某事辩护（中性偏正式）

> You and I, as citizens, have the obligation to shape the debates of our time—not only with the votes we cast, but with the voices we lift in defense of our most ancient values and enduring ideals.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 不只是军事防御。

### 52. answer the call of sth.

**意思：** 响应某事的召唤（正式／演讲）

> With common effort and common purpose, with passion and dedication, let us answer the call of history and carry into an uncertain future that precious light of freedom.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P029](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201300032/html/DCPD-201300032.htm)

**补充：** 原句 history 是修辞；常用在使命或责任，非普通电话。

## 2015-03-07 · 奥巴马：塞尔玛民权游行五十周年讲话

### 1. It is a rare honor to do sth.

**意思：** 能做某事是难得的荣幸（正式／感谢）

> It is a rare honor in this life to follow one of your heroes.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可用于介绍嘉宾或承接机会；rare 强调难得。

### 2. I have to imagine that 从句

**意思：** 我想……应该是如此（中性／推测）

> Now, I have to imagine that when a younger John Lewis woke up that morning 50 years ago and made his way to Brown Chapel, heroics were not on his mind.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句从历史人物处境作推测，非已核实心理事实。

### 3. make sb.'s way to sth.

**意思：** 前往某地（中性／叙事）

> Now, I have to imagine that when a younger John Lewis woke up that morning 50 years ago and made his way to Brown Chapel, heroics were not on his mind.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 常含过程或行进意味；不等同 make way for。

### 4. on sb.'s mind

**意思：** 在某人心里惦记着（中性／生活）

> Now, I have to imagine that when a younger John Lewis woke up that morning 50 years ago and made his way to Brown Chapel, heroics were not on his mind.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句否定表示当时未考虑英雄行为。

### 5. mill about

**意思：** 聚在一处四下走动（中性／叙事）

> Young folks with bedrolls and backpacks were milling about.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 通常群体无明确方向地移动。

### 6. scribble down sth.

**意思：** 匆忙记下某事（中性／生活工作）

> A doctor described what tear gas does to the body, while marchers scribbled down instructions for contacting their loved ones.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处 instructions；scribble 表潦草或快速。

### 7. sb.'s loved ones

**意思：** 亲近的家人或所爱之人（中性／生活）

> A doctor described what tear gas does to the body, while marchers scribbled down instructions for contacting their loved ones.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不限定恋人。

### 8. be thick with sth.

**意思：** 充满某种情绪或事物（文学／叙事）

> The air was thick with doubt, anticipation, and fear.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句空气充满怀疑、期待与害怕；不声称普通口语高频。

### 9. behind bars

**意思：** 在监狱里；被监禁（中性／新闻叙事）

> And then, his knapsack stocked with an apple, a toothbrush, and a book on government—all you need for a night behind bars—John Lewis led them out of the church on a mission to change America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** bars 为牢房铁栏，不是酒吧。

### 10. on a mission to do sth.

**意思：** 怀着明确使命去做某事（中性／行动）

> And then, his knapsack stocked with an apple, a toothbrush, and a book on government—all you need for a night behind bars—John Lewis led them out of the church on a mission to change America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可用于认真目标，随意场景也可能幽默夸张。

### 11. in isolation

**意思：** 孤立地；脱离相关背景地（中性偏正式／分析）

> Now, as is true across the landscape of American history, we cannot examine this moment in isolation.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句反对孤立分析历史事件。

### 12. part of a broader sth.

**意思：** 某个更广泛事物的一部分（中性／界定范围）

> The march on Selma was part of a broader campaign that spanned generations, the leaders that day part of a long line of heroes.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可说明事件属于更大过程，非固定习语。

### 13. stay true to sth.

**意思：** 始终忠于某个原则或目标（中性／立场）

> men and women who despite the gush of blood and splintered bone would stay true to their north star and keep marching towards justice.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 与 remain faithful to 接近，合并时可作为变体。

### 14. in the days to come

**意思：** 在接下来的日子里（中性偏正式／叙事）

> They did as Scripture instructed: "Rejoice in hope, be patient in tribulation, be constant in prayer." And in the days to come, they went back again and again.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不等于过去那些天。

### 15. In time

**意思：** 过一段时间；最终（中性／叙事）

> In time, their chorus would well up and reach President Johnson.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是及时赶到的 in time 义。

### 16. seek special treatment

**意思：** 寻求特殊待遇（中性／评价）

> But they didn't seek special treatment, just the equal treatment promised to them almost a century before.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句否定，强调争取平等待遇。

### 17. Back then

**意思：** 那时候；当年（中性／叙事）

> Back then, they were called Communists or half-breeds or outside agitators, sexual and moral degenerates, and worse.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 承接已提到的过去时期。

### 18. everything but sth.

**意思：** 除了某物以外什么都……（中性／强调）

> They were called everything but the name their parents gave them.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句表达各式辱称却不叫本名；but 为 except。

### 19. What could be more + 形容词 + than sth.?

**意思：** 还有什么比……更……？（论述／反问）

> And yet what could be more American than what happened in this place?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 用于强调极高评价，不是求事实答案。

### 20. It is in sb.'s power to do sth.

**意思：** 某人有能力或权力做某事（正式／能力界限）

> it is in our power to remake this Nation to more closely align with our highest ideals?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处强调公民能够改变国家，非必然成功。

### 21. align with sth.

**意思：** 与某种目标或标准一致（中性／工作）

> it is in our power to remake this Nation to more closely align with our highest ideals?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处 ideals；非只物理对齐。

### 22. That's why 从句

**意思：** 这就是为什么……（中性／解释）

> That's why their Selma is not some outlier in the American experience.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 连接前面的理由和结果，不据易懂而排除。

### 23. a call to action

**意思：** 行动号召（中性偏正式）

> They're a living thing, a call to action, a roadmap for citizenship, and an insistence in the capacity of free men and women to shape our own destiny.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不是 call 的单独电话义。

### 24. engage sb. in sth.

**意思：** 让某人参与某事（中性／工作）

> the success of our experiment in self-government rested on engaging all of our citizens in this work.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处积极参与自治工作。

### 25. That's what sth. is all about

**意思：** 这正是某事的核心意义（中性／解释）

> That's what this movement was all about, one leg in our long journey toward freedom.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** all about 不指所有细节。

### 26. pick up the torch

**意思：** 接过前人的事业继续做（正式／演讲比喻）

> The American instinct that led these young men and women to pick up the torch and cross this bridge, that's the same instinct that moved patriots to choose revolution over tyranny.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不是每个普通工作交接都适用。

### 27. a work in progress

**意思：** 尚在完善中的事物（中性／工作评价）

> It's the idea held by generations of citizens who believed that America is a constant work in progress, who believed that loving this country requires more than singing its praises or avoiding uncomfortable truths.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可用于产品、项目或自身成长。

### 28. sing sb.'s/sth.'s praises

**意思：** 大力赞扬某人／事（中性／评价）

> It's the idea held by generations of citizens who believed that America is a constant work in progress, who believed that loving this country requires more than singing its praises or avoiding uncomfortable truths.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不是字面唱歌；有时可带过度吹捧意味。

### 29. speak out for sth.

**意思：** 公开发声支持某事（中性／立场）

> It requires the occasional disruption, the willingness to speak out for what is right, to shake up the status quo.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** speak out 常强调不保持沉默。

### 30. shake up the status quo

**意思：** 冲击或改变现有局面（中性偏正式／改革）

> It requires the occasional disruption, the willingness to speak out for what is right, to shake up the status quo.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** shake up 并非简单摇晃。

### 31. draw strength from sth.

**意思：** 从某事中获得力量／支持（中性／鼓励）

> From the streets of Tunis to the Maidan in Ukraine, this generation of young people can draw strength from this place, where the powerless could change the world's greatest power and push their leaders to expand the boundaries of freedom.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处历史事件给予继续行动的勇气。

### 32. cement sb.'s reputation as sth.

**意思：** 巩固作为某种角色的声誉（正式／评价）

> That's what cements our reputation as a beacon of opportunity.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** cement 为比喻巩固；非水泥义。

### 33. submit to sth.

**意思：** 屈从于某种统治或要求（正式／立场）

> Young people in Burma went to prison rather than submit to military rule.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处军事统治，非提交文件。

### 34. Which leads us to ask ...

**意思：** 这就引出我们要问的问题……（论述／衔接）

> Which leads us to ask, just how might we repay that debt?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** which 回指前述整件事，不应当作独立完整句模板。

### 35. First and foremost

**意思：** 首先且最重要的是（中性／排序）

> First and foremost, we have to recognize that one day's commemoration, no matter how special, is not enough.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 兼有首要重要性，不仅是时间先后。

### 36. If sth. taught us anything, it's that 从句

**意思：** 如果某事教给我们什么，那就是……（中性／经验总结）

> If Selma taught us anything, it's that our work is never done.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 强调最重要教训，非怀疑有没有学到东西。

### 37. when it comes to sth.

**意思：** 说到某个领域；涉及某事时（中性／界定议题）

> For when it comes to the pursuit of justice, we can afford neither complacency nor despair.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** to 为介词，后可接 doing。

### 38. with respect to sth.

**意思：** 关于某事；就某方面而言（正式／分析）

> Just this week, I was asked whether I thought the Department of Justice's Ferguson report shows that, with respect to race, little has changed in this country.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是尊敬某事。

### 39. reject the notion that 从句

**意思：** 不接受……这一观点（正式／异议）

> But I rejected the notion that nothing's changed.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** notion 引出被反对的主张。

### 40. I was asked whether 从句

**意思：** 有人问我是否……（中性／转述）

> Just this week, I was asked whether I thought the Department of Justice's Ferguson report shows that, with respect to race, little has changed in this country.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 被动引入别人提出的问题，不需添加提问者。

### 41. do a disservice to sb./sth.

**意思：** 对某人／事业帮倒忙或造成不公（中性偏正式／批评）

> We do a disservice to the cause of justice by intimating that bias and discrimination are immutable, that racial division is inherent in America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句指把歧视说成永恒不变反而损害正义。

### 42. live through sth.

**意思：** 亲身经历某段时期或困难（中性／叙事）

> If you think nothing's changed in the past 50 years, ask somebody who lived through the Selma or Chicago or Los Angeles of the 1950s.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不同于住在某处。

### 43. rob sb. of sth.

**意思：** 剥夺某人的某物／机会（中性／影响）

> To deny this progress, this hard-won progress—our progress—would be to rob us of our own agency, our own capacity, our responsibility to do what we can to make America better.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可抽象搭配 agency、chance，不只财物抢劫。

### 44. an isolated incident

**意思：** 孤立个案（中性／分析）

> Of course, a more common mistake is to suggest that Ferguson is an isolated incident, that racism is banished, that the work that drew men and women to Selma is now complete, and that whatever racial tensions remain are a consequence of those seeking to play the "race card" for their own purposes.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句反对把事件说成个案；非 isolated 人际孤独义。

### 45. for sb.'s own purposes

**意思：** 为自己的目的（中性／动机批评）

> those seeking to play the "race card" for their own purposes.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句带借题发挥的负面评价。

### 46. cast a shadow upon/over sth.

**意思：** 给某事蒙上阴影（中性偏文学／影响）

> We just need to open our eyes and our ears and our hearts to know that this Nation's racial history still casts its long shadow upon us.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 long 加强持续历史影响；可迁移但保留比喻语气。

### 47. We don't need sth. to know that 从句

**意思：** 不用某种证据／条件我们也知道……（中性／论证）

> We don't need a Ferguson report to know that's not true.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可能是强势立场，不能自动替代实际查证。

### 48. face up to sth.

**意思：** 正视并承认某事（中性／面对问题）

> We know that reaching that blessed destination where we are judged, all of us, by the content of our character requires admitting as much, facing up to the truth.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 truth；不等同 face down 的对抗。

### 49. be capable of doing sth.

**意思：** 有能力做某事（中性／能力）

> "We are capable of bearing a great burden," James Baldwin once wrote, "once we discover that the burden is reality and arrive where reality is."

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 Baldwin 引文，须保留引文语境。

### 50. look squarely at sth.

**意思：** 直面某个问题（中性偏正式／决心）

> There's nothing America can't handle if we actually look squarely at the problem.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P029](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不是物理看成正方形。

### 51. build consensus

**意思：** 建立共识（中性／工作）

> And if we make such an effort, no matter how hard it may sometimes seem, laws can be passed and consciences can be stirred and consensus can be built.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P029](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句被动 consensus can be built，非统一全部意见。

### 52. There's nothing sb. can't handle if 从句

**意思：** 只要……，某人没有应付不了的事（中性／鼓励）

> There's nothing America can't handle if we actually look squarely at the problem.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P029](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 双重否定强化信心，原句条件是直面问题；不是事实保证。

### 53. risk sb.'s life to do sth.

**意思：** 冒生命危险做某事（中性／叙事）

> Together, we can raise the level of mutual trust that policing is built on: the idea that police officers are members of the community they risk their lives to protect.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P030](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 用于真实重大危险，不泛化为普通工作风险。

### 54. a free ride

**意思：** 不承担成本或责任而享受好处（中性／批评）

> Americans don't accept a free ride for anybody, nor do we believe in equality of outcomes.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P031](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处并非免费乘车。

### 55. give lip service to sth.

**意思：** 口头支持某事却不付诸行动（中性／批评）

> And if we really mean it, if we're not just giving lip service to it, but if we really mean it and are willing to sacrifice for it,

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P031](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不可把 lip service 当积极认可。

### 56. if sb. really means it

**意思：** 如果某人当真／是认真说的（中性／求诚意）

> And if we really mean it, if we're not just giving lip service to it, but if we really mean it and are willing to sacrifice for it,

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P031](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句与实际牺牲连接，非 mean 单词释义。

### 57. As we speak

**意思：** 就在此刻；我们说话的同时（中性／强调当下）

> As we speak, more such laws are being proposed.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P032](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不是就说话方式而言。

### 58. blood, sweat and tears

**意思：** 艰辛付出与牺牲（中性偏演讲）

> Meanwhile, the Voting Rights Act, the culmination of so much blood, so much sweat and tears, the product of so much sacrifice in the face of wanton violence, the Voting Rights Act stands weakened, its future subject to political rancor.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P032](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 so much 插入各名词；不认为字面每项都出现。

### 59. How can that be?

**意思：** 怎么会这样？（中性／惊讶质疑）

> How can that be?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P033](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处反对现状，非仅请求说明。

### 60. make it sb.'s mission to do sth.

**意思：** 把做某事当作自己的使命（中性偏正式／决心）

> If we want to honor this day, let that hundred go back to Washington and gather 400 more, and together, pledge to make it their mission to restore that law this year.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P033](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 语气比 take on a task 更强。

### 61. strike down a law

**意思：** 裁定某法律无效（正式／法律）

> If every new voter suppression law was struck down today, we would still have, here in America, one of the lowest voting rates among free peoples.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P034](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句被动 struck down；不是普通否决建议。

### 62. What's sb.'s excuse for doing/not doing sth.?

**意思：** 某人做／不做某事有什么理由？（中性／质问）

> What's our excuse today for not voting?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P035](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 常带反诘责备，非礼貌中性询问。

### 63. take the time to do sth.

**意思：** 抽出时间认真做某事（中性／工作生活）

> Why are we pointing to somebody else when we could take the time just to go to the polling places?

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P035](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 不只是耗时。

### 64. take sth. for granted

**意思：** 把某事当作理所当然（中性／反思）

> We take for granted conveniences that our parents could have scarcely imagined.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P036](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 这里珍惜便利，非假设一条命题为真。

### 65. That's what it means to do sth.

**意思：** 这才是做某事的含义（中性／阐释）

> That's what it means to love America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P036](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 可迁移到职责、信任等概念，原政治对象不固定。

### 66. sth. is what sb. makes of it

**意思：** 某事会怎样取决于某人如何塑造它（中性／鼓励）

> We know America is what we make of it.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P037](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 America is what we make of it，非声称完全可控。

### 67. and then some

**意思：** 而且还不止如此；甚至更多（口语／强调）

> We are Sojourner Truth and Fannie Lou Hamer, women who could do as much as any man and then some.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P039](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 跟在比较数量或能力后，非然后的一些东西。

### 68. stow away

**意思：** 偷乘交通工具（中性／叙事）

> We're the immigrants who stowed away on ships to reach these shores, the huddled masses yearning to breathe free: Holocaust survivors, Soviet defectors, the Lost Boys of Sudan.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P040](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 on ships；不是一般收纳义。

### 69. even as 从句

**意思：** 正当……之际却……（中性偏正式／让步）

> And we're the Tuskeegee Airmen and the Navajo code talkers and the Japanese Americans who fought for this country even as their own liberty had been denied.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P042](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句一边为国家作战，一边自身自由被剥夺，时间与反差兼有。

### 70. sign up to do sth.

**意思：** 报名／加入以做某事（中性／工作生活）

> We're the firefighters who rushed into those buildings on 9/11, the volunteers who signed up to fight in Afghanistan and Iraq.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P043](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处参军；不把战争内容写成默认对象。

### 71. give voice to sb./sth.

**意思：** 替某人发声；表达某种感受或观点（中性偏正式）

> We are storytellers, writers, poets, artists who abhor unfairness and despise hypocrisy and give voice to the voiceless and tell truths that need to be told.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P043](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 voiceless 指难以让声音被听到的人。

### 72. stand fast

**意思：** 坚定立场，毫不动摇（正式／文学）

> We are the people Langston Hughes wrote of who "build our temples for tomorrow, strong as we know how." We are the people Emerson wrote of, "who for truth and honor's sake stand fast and suffer long," who are "never tired, so long as we can see far enough."

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P046](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原 Emerson 引文，非站得快。

### 73. pine for sth.

**意思：** 苦苦怀念或渴望某物（中性偏文学／情绪）

> We respect the past, but we don't pine for the past.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P047](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句否定沉湎过去。

### 74. at the ripe old age of ...

**意思：** 在……的高龄（口语／幽默）

> That's why someone like John Lewis at the ripe old age of 25 could lead a mighty march.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P047](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句25岁形成反讽，不按真实高龄理解。

### 75. take away sth. from sth.

**意思：** 从某次经历中领悟／获得某种认识（中性／总结）

> And that's what the young people here today and listening all across the country must take away from this day.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P048](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 what ... must take away，区别带走物品义。

### 76. new ground to cover

**意思：** 尚待探索的新领域或工作（中性／工作比喻）

> For everywhere in this country, there are first steps to be taken, there's new ground to cover, there are more bridges to be crossed.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P049](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句与桥梁构成演讲意象，不收整段为固定表达。

### 77. hold firmly to sth.

**意思：** 牢牢坚持某种信念或准则（中性偏正式）

> When it feels the road is too hard, when the torch we've been passed feels too heavy, we will remember these early travelers and draw strength from their example and hold firmly to the words of the prophet Isaiah:

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P051](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 原句 words 为坚持其中信念，不是物理握住。

### 78. no longer with us

**意思：** 已不在人世（中性／委婉）

> May He bless those warriors of justice no longer with us and bless the United States of America.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P057](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201500157/html/DCPD-201500157.htm)

**补充：** 此处指逝去的民权人士；其他语境也可只指不在身边。

## 2017-01-07 · 奥巴马：告别演讲前的每周讲话

### 1. start a family

**意思：** 成家并开始养育孩子（中性／生活）

> I chose Chicago not only because it's my hometown, where I met my wife and we started a family, but also because it's really where my career in public service began.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 不一定仅指结婚；原句与 met my wife 区分。

### 2. a running thread through sth.

**意思：** 贯穿某事的持续线索或主题（中性偏正式／回顾）

> The running thread through my career has been the notion that when ordinary people get involved and get engaged and come together in collective effort, things change for the better.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句贯穿职业历程，不是软件线程。

### 3. get involved

**意思：** 参与进去（中性／行动）

> The running thread through my career has been the notion that when ordinary people get involved and get engaged and come together in collective effort, things change for the better.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 后可接 in；此处普通人的公共参与。

### 4. change for the better

**意思：** 向好的方向改变（中性／评价）

> The running thread through my career has been the notion that when ordinary people get involved and get engaged and come together in collective effort, things change for the better.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** better 表结果方向，不只单纯变化。

### 5. at the heart of sth.

**意思：** 在某事的核心；是某事关键（中性／解释）

> That's the belief at the heart of this precious American experiment in self-government.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 不是生理心脏。

### 6. lose sight of sth.

**意思：** 忽略或忘了某事的重要性（中性／反思）

> It's easy to lose sight of that truth in the day-to-day back and forth of Washington or our minute-to-minute news cycles.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 非视力失去。

### 7. back and forth

**意思：** 来回反复；拉锯往复（中性）

> It's easy to lose sight of that truth in the day-to-day back and forth of Washington or our minute-to-minute news cycles.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句名词化的政治日常往复，也可字面来回。

### 8. over a longer time horizon

**意思：** 放到更长的时间跨度来看（正式／规划分析）

> But remember that America is a story told over a longer time horizon, in fits and starts, punctuated at times by hardship, but ultimately written by generations of citizens who've somehow worked together, without fanfare, to form a more perfect Union.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** horizon 比喻观察或计划期限。

### 9. in fits and starts

**意思：** 断断续续；进展不连贯（中性／过程）

> But remember that America is a story told over a longer time horizon, in fits and starts, punctuated at times by hardship, but ultimately written by generations of citizens who've somehow worked together, without fanfare, to form a more perfect Union.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 不是一次顺畅的前进。

### 10. be punctuated by sth.

**意思：** 过程中不时穿插某事（正式／叙事）

> But remember that America is a story told over a longer time horizon, in fits and starts, punctuated at times by hardship, but ultimately written by generations of citizens who've somehow worked together, without fanfare, to form a more perfect Union.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句 by hardship，并非标点教学义。

### 11. without fanfare

**意思：** 不事张扬地；没有大肆宣扬（中性偏正式）

> But remember that America is a story told over a longer time horizon, in fits and starts, punctuated at times by hardship, but ultimately written by generations of citizens who've somehow worked together, without fanfare, to form a more perfect Union.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 不只无喇叭声。

### 12. turn sth. into sth.

**意思：** 把某事转变成另一种状态（中性／成果说明）

> Together, we've turned an economy that was shrinking and losing jobs into one that's growing and creating jobs, with poverty falling, incomes rising, and wages that have jumped faster over the past few years than at any time in the past four decades.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句从萎缩经济转为增长经济，保留转变方向。

### 13. faster than at any time in ...

**意思：** 比过去……任何时候都快（中性／趋势比较）

> Together, we've turned an economy that was shrinking and losing jobs into one that's growing and creating jobs, with poverty falling, incomes rising, and wages that have jumped faster over the past few years than at any time in the past four decades.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 应有事实数据支持比较；不把演讲主张视为独立核证。

### 14. a preexisting condition

**意思：** 事先已经存在的疾病／状况（正式／保险医疗）

> ending the days of discrimination against up to half of Americans who have a preexisting condition, and secured new rights and protections for everybody with health insurance.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 此处医疗保险语境，不将单词 preexisting 包装独立chunk。

### 15. for good

**意思：** 永久地；彻底地（中性）

> Together, we've brought home most of our brave troops from Iraq and Afghanistan, took bin Laden and thousands of other terrorists off the battlefield for good.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句 off the battlefield for good，不是为了好处。

### 16. open a new chapter with sb.

**意思：** 与某人开启新阶段（正式／回顾）

> We've opened a new chapter with the people of Cuba, shut down Iran's nuclear weapons program without firing a single shot, and brought the world together around a climate agreement that could save this planet for future generations.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P006](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句外交关系，比日常 start again 更庄重。

### 17. by these measures

**意思：** 按这些衡量标准（正式／总结）

> By these measures and many more, we have made America a better, stronger place for the generation that follows ours.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** measures 此处标准或指标，不是行动措施。

### 18. run sb.'s leg (of a journey/relay)

**意思：** 完成自己负责的一段接力／路程（演讲／接力比喻）

> We've run our leg in a long journey of progress, knowing that our work is and will always be unfinished.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句 long journey，非所有项目默认套语。

### 19. tune in

**意思：** 收看／收听节目（中性／邀请）

> That will be the focus of my Farewell to you this Tuesday, and I hope you'll tune in.

**来源：** [Barack Obama · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700007/html/DCPD-201700007.htm)

**补充：** 原句邀请听告别演讲，不是调整乐器音准。

## 2017-01-20 · 特朗普：2017 年就职演说

### 1. get the job done

**意思：** 把任务完成；重点在结果（日常／工作）

> We will face challenges, we will confront hardships, but we will get the job done.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** job 可以是具体任务，不一定是职业岗位；不自动表示完成得好。

> It isn't perfect, but it gets the job done.（教学自造）

### 2. for years to come

**意思：** 在未来许多年里（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> Together, we will determine the course of America and the world for many, many years to come.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 3. carry out sth.

**意思：** 执行／开展某事（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> Every 4 years, we gather on these steps to carry out the orderly and peaceful transfer of power, and we are grateful to President Obama and First Lady Michelle Obama for their gracious aid throughout this transition.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 4. not merely ... but ...

**意思：** 不只是……，而是……（正式／重定焦点）

> Today's ceremony, however, has very special meaning. Because today we are not merely transferring power from one administration to another or from one party to another, but we are transferring power from Washington, DC, and giving it back to you, the people.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 强调后半部分更重要的性质。

### 5. reap the rewards of sth.

**意思：** 获得某事带来的回报（中性偏正式／评价）

> For too long, a small group in our Nation's Capital has reaped the rewards of Government while the people have borne the cost.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 6. bear the cost

**意思：** 承担代价（中性偏正式／评价）

> For too long, a small group in our Nation's Capital has reaped the rewards of Government while the people have borne the cost.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 7. share in sth.

**意思：** 分享／分得某事的成果（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> Washington flourished, but the people did not share in its wealth.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 8. while ... there is little to ...

**意思：** 虽然／当……，却没有多少值得……的事（正式／对照评价）

> Their victories have not been your victories; their triumphs have not been your triumphs; and while they celebrated in our Nation's Capital, there was little to celebrate for struggling families all across our land.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 原句 while 表并存对比；槽位须保留有意义的评价动作。

### 9. What truly matters is not ... but whether ...

**意思：** 真正重要的不是……，而是是否……（中性偏正式／澄清标准）

> What truly matters is not which party controls our Government, but whether our Government is controlled by the people.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 10. At the center of sth. is ...

**意思：** 某事的核心是……（正式／说明核心）

> At the center of this movement is a crucial conviction: that a nation exists to serve its citizens.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 11. the likes of which ... has never seen before

**意思：** ……从未见过的那种……（正式／强调罕见规模）

> You came by the tens of millions to become part of a historic movement the likes of which the world has never seen before.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 较长修饰骨架，先行词为 movement；原句数量主张未核证。

### 12. be deprived of sth.

**意思：** 被剥夺／缺乏某物（中性偏正式／处境）

> But for too many of our citizens, a different reality exists: Mothers and children trapped in poverty in our inner cities; rusted-out factories scattered like tombstones across the landscape of our Nation; an education system, flush with cash, but which leaves our young and beautiful students deprived of all knowledge; and the crime and the gangs and the drugs that have stolen too many lives and robbed our country of so much unrealized potential.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 原句 leaves ... deprived of all knowledge；不认定其教育评价。

### 13. at the expense of sth.

**意思：** 以牺牲某事为代价（中性偏正式／代价对比）

> For many decades, we've enriched foreign industry at the expense of American industry, subsidized the armies of other countries while allowing for the very sad depletion of our military.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 14. fall into disrepair

**意思：** 失修而破败（中性偏正式／描述设施）

> We've defended other nations' borders while refusing to defend our own and spent trillions and trillions of dollars overseas while America's infrastructure has fallen into disrepair and decay.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 15. one by one

**意思：** 一个接一个地（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> One by one, the factories shuttered and left our shores, with not even a thought about the millions and millions of American workers that were left behind.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 16. from this day forward

**意思：** 从今天起（正式／宣布新阶段）

> From this day forward, a new vision will govern our land.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 原段第二次 this 重复保留，不改原句。

### 17. let sb. down

**意思：** 让某人失望；辜负其期待（日常）

> I will fight for you with every breath in my body, and I will never, ever let you down.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 此处不是物理上放下人；ever 强化原句的承诺。

> I know you were counting on me, and I'm sorry I let you down.（教学自造）

### 18. get sb. back to work

**意思：** 让某人重新开始工作（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> We will get our people off of welfare and back to work, rebuilding our country with American hands and American labor.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 19. with the understanding that 从句

**意思：** 以……这一共同理解／前提为基础（中性偏正式／说明前提）

> We will seek friendship and good will with the nations of the world, but we do so with the understanding that it is the right of all nations to put their own interests first.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 20. put sb.'s own interests first

**意思：** 把自己的利益放在首位（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> We will seek friendship and good will with the nations of the world, but we do so with the understanding that it is the right of all nations to put their own interests first.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 21. no room for [X]

**意思：** 没有容纳 X 的余地；某种态度或行为不应存在（中性／立场表达）

> When you open your heart to patriotism, there is no room for prejudice.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 可字面指空间，也可抽象表达不容许；no room for error 表示不允许出错。

> There's no room for personal attacks in this discussion.（教学自造）

### 22. speak sb.'s mind

**意思：** 坦率说出自己的想法（中性／观点、工作或叙事）

> We must speak our minds openly, debate our disagreements honestly, but always pursue solidarity.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 23. all talk and no action

**意思：** 光说不做；只会嘴上说（日常／直接批评）

> We will no longer accept politicians who are all talk and no action, constantly complaining, but never doing anything about it.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 带明确负面评价；评价同事时可能显得尖锐。

> He makes big promises, but he's all talk and no action.（教学自造）

### 24. think big

**意思：** 大胆设想／有大志向（中性／鼓励）

> Finally, we must think big and dream even bigger.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 原句 dream even bigger 是同句修辞延伸。

### 25. Do not allow anyone to tell you that ...

**意思：** 不要让任何人告诉你……（中性／鼓励或反驳）

> Do not allow anyone to tell you that it cannot be done.

**来源：** [Donald Trump · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-201700058/html/DCPD-201700058.htm)

**补充：** 按原句语境记录可替换槽位；候选待核，不以演讲出现推定日常高频。

### 26. whether ... or ..., 从句

**意思：** 无论是……还是……，都……（中性／包容与共同结果）
