> Folks, I'm going to get in trouble for saying that, but any of you want to get in Air Force One with me and fly to Toronto, Berlin, Moscow—I mean, excuse me.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P077](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 33. a very big deal

**意思：** 一件非常重要的事（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> Folks, the Affordable Care Act—the old "Obamacare"—is still a very big deal.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P078](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语；deal此处非交易。

### 34. I'm not going to let that happen

**意思：** 我不会让那件事发生（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The President. I'm not going to let that happen.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P080](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 坚定阻止；that须有前文。

### 35. put sth. toward sth.

**意思：** 把钱用于某一支出（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> But I'm not waiting. I want to provide an annual tax credit that will give Americans $400 a month for the next 2 years as mortgage rates come down to put toward their mortgages when they buy their first home or trade up for a little more space. Just for 2 years.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P084](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 36. trade up for sth.

**意思：** 换购更好／更大的东西（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I want to provide an annual tax credit that will give Americans $400 a month for the next 2 years as mortgage rates come down to put toward their mortgages when they buy their first home or trade up for a little more space.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P084](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 37. as a consequence

**意思：** 因此，作为结果（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> When you refinance your home, you can save $1,000 or more as a consequence.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P085](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 38. crack down on sth.

**意思：** 严厉打击／整治某种行为（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> For millions of renters, we're cracking down on big landlords who use antitrust law—using antitrust—who break antitrust laws—by price-fixing and driving up rents. We've cut redtape so builders can get federally financing, which is already helping build a record 1.7 million new house—housing units nationwide.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P086](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 39. cut redtape

**意思：** 减少繁文缛节（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We've cut redtape so builders can get federally financing, which is already helping build a record 1.7 million new house—housing units nationwide.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P086](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原件redtape合写照录；正常教学核验red tape。

### 40. give sb. a good start

**意思：** 让某人有良好的起点（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And I, like I suspect all of you, want to give a child—every child a good start by providing access to preschool for 3- and 4-years-old.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P088](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 41. point out sth.

**意思：** 指出某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You know, I think I pointed out last year—[applause]. I think I pointed out last year that children coming from broken homes where there's no books, they're not read to, not spoken to very often start school—kindergarten or first grade hearing—having heard a million fewer words spoken.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P089](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 42. go on to do sth.

**意思：** 随后进一步做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Well, studies show that children who go to preschool are nearly 50-percent more likely to finish high school and go on to earn a 2- and 4-year degree no matter what their background is.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P090](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 不同于继续做同一件事。

### 43. Don't hold me to the exact number

**意思：** 别要求我对这个确切数字打包票（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I pointed out to them: As Vice President, I met with over eight—I think it was 182 of those folks—don't hold me to the exact number—and I asked them what they need most—the CEOs.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P092](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 说明数字为记忆中的估计。

### 44. on both sides of the aisle

**意思：** 在双方阵营中（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And you've had the same experience on both sides of the aisle.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P092](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原指议会两党，职场使用需语境明确。

### 45. hands-on experience

**意思：** 实际动手的经验（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I want to expand high-quality tutoring and summer learning to see that every child learns to read by third grade. I'm also connecting local businesses and high schools so students get hands- on experience and a path to a good-paying job whether or not they go to college.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P095](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 46. whether or not 从句

**意思：** 无论是否……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I'm also connecting local businesses and high schools so students get hands- on experience and a path to a good-paying job whether or not they go to college.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P095](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 47. reduce the burden of sth.

**意思：** 减轻某事造成的负担（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> When I was told I couldn't universally just change the way in which we did—dealt with student loans, I fixed two student loan programs that already existed to reduce the burden of student debt for nearly 4 million Americans, including nurses, firefighters, and others in public service.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P097](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 48. while we're at it

**意思：** 既然已经在做这事，就顺便……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And while we're at it, I want to give public school teachers a raise.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P100](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 引入相关追加事项。

### 49. give sb. a raise

**意思：** 给某人加薪（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And while we're at it, I want to give public school teachers a raise. And by the way, the first couple of years, we cut the deficit.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P100](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 50. speak to the question of sth.

**意思：** 回应／论述某个问题（正式／演讲）

> Now let me speak to the question of fundamental fairness for all Americans.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P101](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 正式；speak to非对物说话。

### 51. pay sb.'s fair share

**意思：** 承担自己公平的一份（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Just pay your fair share in taxes.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P103](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原句为纳税，也可用于共同费用。

### 52. Here's the deal

**意思：** 情况是这样的／重点是……（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> But here's the deal.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P105](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语引入要点，非总指交易。

### 53. Check the numbers

**意思：** 看看数据／把数核对一下（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> They added more to the national debt than any Presidential term in American history. Check the numbers.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P108](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 用证据核实判断的指令。

### 54. fight like hell to do sth.

**意思：** 拼尽全力去做某事（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> The President. I sure don't. I'm going to keep fighting like hell to make it fair. Under my plan, nobody earning less than $400,000 a year will pay an additional penny in Federal taxes— nobody—not one penny. And they haven't yet.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P113](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语强烈，hell不宜正式温和沟通。

### 55. cut sth. in half

**意思：** 把数量减少一半（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> In fact, the child tax credit I passed during the pandemic cut taxes for millions of working families and cut child poverty in half. Restore that child tax credit. No child should go hungry in this country.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P114](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 56. No one should have to choose between A and B

**意思：** 不该让任何人被迫在A与B之间选择（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Imagine a future with paid leave, because no one should have to choose between working and taking care of their sick family member.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P126](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 规范立场，比don't have to更强调不应制造困境。

### 57. on the chopping block

**意思：** 面临被削减／取消的危险（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Many of my friends on the other side of the aisle want to put Social Security on the chopping block.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P127](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 比喻；原文为社保。

### 58. I kind of thought 从句

**意思：** 我本来有点以为……（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> The President. I kind of thought that's what your plan was.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P133](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语缓和断言，也可含讽刺。

### 59. That's good to hear

**意思：** 听到这个真好（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> [Laughter] Well, that's good to hear.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P133](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 接收消息的回应；原场景含调侃。

### 60. pad sb.'s profits

**意思：** 人为增加／虚增利润（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The President. ——too many corporations raise prices to pad their profits, charging more and more for less and less. That's why we're cracking down on corporations that engage in price gouging and deceptive pricing, from food to health care to housing.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P136](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 负面评价，勿用于中性利润增长。

### 61. a hell of a lot

**意思：** 非常多／很大程度（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> In fact, the snack companies think you won't notice if they change the size of the bag and put a hell of a lot fewer—[laughter]—same size bag—put fewer chips in it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P137](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语粗俗加强语。

### 62. without sb.'s knowledge

**意思：** 在某人不知情的情况下（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Look, I'm also getting rid of junk fees, those hidden fees at the end of your bill that are there without your knowledge.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P139](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 63. up front

**意思：** 事先，开门见山地（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I'm not stopping there. My administration has proposed rules to make cable, travel, utilities, and online ticket sellers tell you the total price up front so there are no surprises. It matters. It matters.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P143](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 本句事先告知总价，非预付义。

### 64. I'm not stopping there

**意思：** 我还不止做这些（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I'm not stopping there.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P143](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 承接已有举措再追加。

### 65. I'll be darned

**意思：** 真没想到／真奇怪（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> Oh, you don't like that bill—huh?—that conservatives got together and said was a good bill? I'll be darned.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P148](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语惊讶，darn为缓和damn；此处讽刺。

### 66. given the opportunity

**意思：** 如果有机会／在得到机会的条件下（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I believe that given the opportunity—for—a majority in the House and Senate would endorse the bill as well—a majority right now.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P155](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 67. My heart goes out to sb.

**意思：** 我深深同情某人（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> To her parents, I say: My heart goes out to you.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P167](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 慰问悲痛者，不当普通轻微不便套语。

### 68. It's highly unlikely that 从句

**意思：** ……极不可能（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> But—but—if it's only 6 months—6 weeks, the idea is it's highly unlikely that people will pay that money and come all that way knowing that they'll be—able to be kicked out quickly.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P170](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 限定可能性判断。

### 69. I would respectfully suggest ...

**意思：** 我谨建议……（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> Folks, I would respectfully suggest to my friend in—my Republican friends owe it to the American people.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P171](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 礼貌提出意见；原句含口语重启，不能整句当模板。

### 70. pressure sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 向某人施压要求其做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The President. And if my predecessor is watching: Instead of paying [playing; White House correction] politics and pressuring Members of Congress to block the bill, join me in telling the Congress to pass it. We can do it together. But that's what he apparently—here's what he will not do.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P174](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 71. Take a look at it

**意思：** 看看它／了解一下它（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Unlike my predecessor, on my first day in office, I introduced a comprehensive bill to fix our immigration system. Take a look at it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P176](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 72. We can ... or we can ...

**意思：** 我们可以……，也可以……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Look, folks, we have a simple choice: We can fight about fixing the border, or we can fix it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P180](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 本句用争论与行动作选择，属于论述骨架而非习语。

### 73. leave sb. for dead

**意思：** 以为某人已死而将其丢下（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> They were beaten. They were bloodied and left for dead. Our late friend and former colleague John Lewis was on that march. We miss him.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P184](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 叙事固定说法，含暴力语境。

### 74. do more than talk

**意思：** 不只说说，要付诸行动（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> But if you truly want to honor him and all the heroes who marched with him, then it's time to do more than talk.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P187](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 75. I have your back

**意思：** 我会支持你、为你撑腰（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> And my message to transgender Americans is, I have your back.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P190](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语保证支持。

### 76. At least, I hope ...

**意思：** 至少我希望……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I don't think any of you think there's no longer a climate crisis. At least, I hope you don't.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P192](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 对先前判断添加保留。

### 77. in the forefront of sth.

**意思：** 处于某领域的最前沿（正式／演讲）

> And patterned after the Peace Corps and AmericaCorps [AmeriCorps; White House correction], I launched the Climate Corps to put 20,000 young people to work in the forefront of our clean energy future.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P194](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 正式。

### 78. have sth. on sb.'s record

**意思：** 让某事留在个人记录上（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Directing my Cabinet to review the Federal classification of marijuana and expunging thousands of convictions for the mere possession, because no one should be jailed for simply using or have it on their record.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P206](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原指犯罪记录，工作语境也须说明记录类型。

### 79. ramp up sth.

**意思：** 加强、扩大某项行动（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Take on crimes of domestic violence. I'm ramping up the Federal enforcement of the Violence Against Women Act that I proudly wrote when I was a Senator so we can finally— finally end the scourge against women in America.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P207](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 80. lead the charge

**意思：** 带头推进／冲在前面（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Well, I did do something by establishing the first ever Office of Gun Violence Prevention in the White House, that the Vice President is leading the charge. Thank you for doing it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P211](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 比喻，不只军事冲锋。

### 81. get over it

**意思：** 放下、走出某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> After another shooting in Iowa recently, he said—when asked what to do about it, he said, just "get over it." That was his quote.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P214](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 引述对手冷漠措辞，悲伤场景可能非常冒犯；非推荐慰问。

### 82. We will not rest until 从句

**意思：** 在……之前我们决不停止努力（正式／演讲）

> I pledge to all the families that we will not rest until we bring every one of your loved ones home.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P220](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 强烈承诺，演讲语域。

### 83. work around the clock

**意思：** 昼夜不停地工作（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We also—we will also work around the clock to bring home Evan and Paul, Americans being unjustly detained by the Russians—and others around the world.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P221](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 84. lay down arms

**意思：** 放下武器、停止战斗（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Israel has a right to go after Hamas. Hamas ended this conflict by releasing the hostages, laying down arms—could end it by releasing the hostages, laying down arms, and surrendering those responsible for October 7.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P222](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 战争语域。

### 85. take a toll on sb./sth.

**意思：** 给……造成伤害／沉重损耗（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Israel also has a fundamental responsibility, though, to protect innocent civilians in Gaza. This war has taken a greater toll on innocent civilians than all previous wars in Gaza combined. More than 30,000 Palestinians have been killed——

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P224](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 86. Says who?

**意思：** 谁说的？／依据是谁？（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Audience member. Says who?

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P225](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 强势质疑来源；来自听众，不是总统。

### 87. build toward sth.

**意思：** 逐步推进到某个目标（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I've been working nonstop to establish an immediate cease-fire that would last for 6 weeks to get all the prisoners released—all the hostages released and to get the hostages home and ease the intolerable and—humanitarian crisis and build toward an enduring—a more—something more enduring.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P228](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原句自我重启保留，不将口误教学。

### 88. do sb.'s part

**意思：** 做好自己应尽的一份（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And Israel must do its part. Israel must allow more aid into Gaza and ensure humanitarian workers aren't caught in the crossfire. And they're announcing they're going to—they're going to have a crossing in Northern Gaza.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P232](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 89. be caught in the crossfire

**意思：** 被卷入交火／双方冲突（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And Israel must do its part. Israel must allow more aid into Gaza and ensure humanitarian workers aren't caught in the crossfire. And they're announcing they're going to—they're going to have a crossing in Northern Gaza.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P232](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 原文实际交火；也可比喻争端波及。

### 90. a bargaining chip

**意思：** 谈判筹码（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> To the leadership of Israel, I say this: Humanitarian assistance cannot be a secondary consideration or a bargaining chip.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P233](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 此处反对把援助作为筹码。

### 91. as a supporter of sth.

**意思：** 以某事终身支持者的身份（正式／演讲）

> And I say this as a lifelong supporter of Israel, my entire career.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P234](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 正式立场身份；泛化程度待教学审校。lifelong为本句修饰语，不是固定骨架。

### 92. not hesitate to do sth.

**意思：** 毫不犹豫地做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> As Commander in Chief, I will not hesitate to direct further measures to protect our people and our military personnel.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P237](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 93. on the rise

**意思：** 正在上升、壮大（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> For years, I've heard many of my Republican and Democratic friends say that China is on the rise and America is falling behind.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P238](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 94. fall behind

**意思：** 落后（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> For years, I've heard many of my Republican and Democratic friends say that China is on the rise and America is falling behind. They've got it backwards. I've been saying it for over 4 years, even when I wasn't President.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P238](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 95. have it backwards

**意思：** 把关系／事实搞反了（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> For years, I've heard many of my Republican and Democratic friends say that China is on the rise and America is falling behind. They've got it backwards. I've been saying it for over 4 years, even when I wasn't President.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P238](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语纠正解释。

### 96. It never occurred to sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 某人从没想到过要做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Frankly, for all his tough talk on China, it never occurred to my predecessor to do any of that.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P241–P242](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** quote跨P241–242，否定思路曾出现，不一定指没能力。

### 97. for that matter

**意思：** 就这方面而言也一样／说起来（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> conflict [competition; White House correction] of the 21st century against China than anyone else for that matter—than at any time as well.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P244](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 追加相近判断；原句重启较多。

### 98. into harm's way

**意思：** 进入危险境地（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And keep our truly sacred obligation to train and equip those we send into harm's way and care for them and their families when they come home and when they don't.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P251](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 多见于人员被派往危险任务。

### 99. as we know it

**意思：** 以我们目前所认识的形式（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And we owe it to ourselves to keep supporting our new health research agency called ARPA–H—and remind us—to remind us that we can do big things, like end cancer as we know it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P253](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 不是简单的正如我们所知。

### 100. owe it to sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 有责任为某人做某事（中性／口语、工作；原素材为演讲）

> And we owe it to ourselves to keep supporting our new health research agency called ARPA–H—and remind us—to remind us that we can do big things, like end cancer as we know it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P253](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 第二遍补充：内部动词词块／义务表达骨架。

### 101. I've been around a while

**意思：** 我经历不少／资历不浅（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> I know I may not look like it, but I've been around a while.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P255](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 口语自我调侃，非一直在附近。

### 102. again and again

**意思：** 一次又一次（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I know the American story. Again and again, I've seen the contest between competing forces in the battle for the soul of our Nation, between those who want to pull America back to the past and those who want to move America into the future.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P256](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 103. see it differently

**意思：** 对此有不同的看法（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Now, other people my age see it differently.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P258](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 104. watch in horror as 从句

**意思：** 惊恐地目睹……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I watched in horror as two of my heroes—like many of you did—Dr. King and Bobby Kennedy, were assassinated. And their legacies inspired me to pursue a career of service.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P260](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 叙事反应骨架。

### 105. by accident

**意思：** 偶然地、并非有意安排（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And I became a county councilman almost by accident.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P261](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 106. have no intention of doing sth.

**意思：** 没有做某事的打算（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I got elected to the United States Senate when I had no intention of running, at age 29. Then Vice President to our first Black President. Now a President to the first woman Vice President. In my career, I've been told I was too young. [Laughter] By the way, they didn't let me on the Senate elevators for votes sometimes. They—not a joke. [Laughter]

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P262](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 107. The issue isn't ..., it's ...

**意思：** 问题不在于……，而在于……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The President. My fellow Americans, the issue facing our Nation isn't how old we are, it's how old are our ideas.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P267](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 重新界定讨论焦点。

### 108. I see a future where 从句

**意思：** 我展望这样一种未来：……（正式／演讲）

> Tonight you've heard mine. I see a future where [we're; White House correction] defending democracy, you don't diminish it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P269](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400170/html/DCPD-202400170.htm)

**补充：** 正式愿景骨架，非只指眼睛看见。

## 2024-11-07 · 拜登：大选后的全国讲话

### 1. Good to see sb.

**意思：** 很高兴看到大家聚在一起（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Thank you. Good to see this Cabinet and staff together here.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P001](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 见面与团体开场；不是认定用户已掌握的基础问候。together是本次聚会情境修饰，不是固定必要部分。

### 2. carry on sth.

**意思：** 继续开展某项活动（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> For over 200 years, America has carried on the greatest experiment in self-government in the history of the world—and that's not hyperbole, that's a fact—where the people—the people—vote and choose their own leaders, and they do it peacefully; and where, in a democracy, the will of the people always prevails.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 3. work with sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 与某人合作来完成……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And I assured him that I would direct my entire administration to work with his team to ensure a peaceful and orderly transition.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 4. early on

**意思：** 在早期；一开始（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> She ran an inspiring campaign, and everyone got to see something that I learned early on to respect so much: her character. She has a backbone like a ramrod. She has great character, true character. She gave her whole heart and effort, and she and her entire team should be proud of the campaign they ran.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 5. to state the obvious

**意思：** 说一句显而易见的话（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I know, for some people, it's a time for victory, to state the obvious.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 话语套语；点明明摆着的事实，有时带强调或讽刺。

### 6. bring down the temperature

**意思：** 缓和紧张局面；给争论降温（中性／公开讨论）

> bring down the temperature.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 这里的 temperature 是冲突强度，不是实际温度；说话时还需把前面的口语长句整理清楚。

> Let's bring down the temperature and focus on what we can fix.（教学自造）

### 7. You can't do sth. only when 从句

**意思：** 不能仅在某种条件满足时才做……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I've said many times you can't love your country only when you win.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 外层立场骨架；不可因此前教材已出现而删去。

### 8. no matter who 从句

**意思：** 不论是谁……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Something I hope we can do no matter who you voted for is see each other not as adversaries, but as fellow Americans, bring down the temperature.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 9. see sb. not as A, but as B

**意思：** 将某人看成B而非A（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Campaigns are contests of competing visions. The country chooses one or the other. And we accept the choice the country made. I've said many times you can't love your country only when you win. You can't love your neighbor only when you agree. Something I hope we can do no matter who you voted for is see each other not as adversaries, but as fellow Americans, bring down the temperature.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P009](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 重新界定看待对象的方式；不是任意名词串。

### 10. lay sth. to rest

**意思：** 消除疑虑、结束争论，使某个问题不再反复被提起（中性偏正式）

> I also hope we can lay to rest the question about the integrity of the American electoral system.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 不等于每一种 solve；常配 doubts、fears、rumors、questions。原句用 lay to rest the question，宾语较长所以后置。

> The new evidence should lay those doubts to rest.（教学自造）

### 11. win or lose

**意思：** 不论输赢；输赢都一样适用（日常／口语与书面）

> And it can be trusted, win or lose.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 可放句首或句尾补充条件；不是句式骨架 only when 的同义替换，而是把相反条件都纳入。

> Win or lose, I'm glad we gave it a try.（教学自造）

### 12. take risks

**意思：** 冒风险（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I also hope we can restore the respect for all our election workers, who busted their necks and took risks at the outset. We should thank them, thank them for staffing voting sites, counting the votes, protecting the very integrity of the election. Many of them are volunteers who do it simply out of love for their country.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 13. at the outset

**意思：** 在开始时（正式／演讲）

> I also hope we can restore the respect for all our election workers, who busted their necks and took risks at the outset.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 较正式的时间套语。

### 14. out of love for sb./sth.

**意思：** 出于对……的爱（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Many of them are volunteers who do it simply out of love for their country.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P011](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 动机结构，不是空间方向。

### 15. do sb.'s duty

**意思：** 尽自己的职责（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And as they did their duty as citizens, I will do my duty as President. I will fulfill my oath, and I will honor the Constitution. On January 20, we'll have a peaceful transfer of power here in America.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 16. hang out with sb.

**意思：** 和某人待在一起、相处（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> To all our incredible staff, supporters, Cabinet members, all the people who have been hanging out with me for the last 4 years—[laughter]—"God love you," as my mother would say—thank you so much.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P013](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 口语；此处幽默地称与自己共事。

### 17. put so much into sth.

**意思：** 为某事投入很多精力（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You put so much into the past 4 years.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 18. I hear you

**意思：** 我听到了你的感受／我理解你（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You're hurting. I hear you, and I see you.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P014](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 此处为认可痛苦；不自动表示同意对方观点。

### 19. kick in

**意思：** 开始发挥作用（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> Much of the work we've done is already being felt by the American people. But the vast majority of it will not be felt—be felt over the next 10 years. We have a—we have legislation we passed that's just—only now just really kicking in.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P016](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 此处法律效果开始显现，口语。

### 20. It takes time to do sth.

**意思：** 做某事需要时间（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We're going to see over a trillion dollars' worth of infrastructure work done, changing people's lives in rural communities and communities that are in real difficulty, because it takes time to get it done, and so much more that's going to take time.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P017](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 21. assuming 从句

**意思：** 假设……；在……前提下（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The road ahead is clear, assuming we sustain it. There's so much—so much—we can get done and will get done based the way the legislation was passed. And it's truly historic.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P018](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 限定判断成立的条件。

### 22. change sth. for the better

**意思：** 使某事向好的方向改变（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Together, we've changed America for the better.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P019](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 23. make every [day/moment] count

**意思：** 让每一天／每一刻都有价值；充分利用时间（日常与鼓励性表达）

> Let's make every day count.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** count 在此是有价值、产生作用，不是计数；不是 every 后可随意装任何名词的万能结构。

> We only have a week left, so let's make every day count.（教学自造）

### 24. get knocked down / get back up

**意思：** 受挫后重新振作（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Look, folks, you all know it in your lives: Setbacks are unavoidable, but giving up is unforgivable. Setbacks are unavoidable, but giving up is unforgivable. We all get knocked down, but the measure of our character, as my dad would say, is how quickly we get back up.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 原文为比喻用法；也可字面指跌倒与起身。

### 25. the measure of sth. is how 从句

**意思：** 衡量……的标准是如何……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We all get knocked down, but the measure of our character, as my dad would say, is how quickly we get back up.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 评判标准骨架，原句含插入语。

### 26. A defeat does not mean ...

**意思：** 一次失败并不意味着……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Remember, a defeat does not mean we are defeated.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 区分一次结果与持续状态；结构候选，非整句政治口号。

### 27. and counting

**意思：** 而且数字还在增加（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> That's the story of America for over 240 years and counting.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P023](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 放在数字后说明仍在累积。

### 28. stay engaged

**意思：** 保持投入、持续参与（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> The American experiment endures, and we're going to be okay, but we need to stay engaged.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 29. keep the faith

**意思：** 保持信心，别因受挫而放弃相信（鼓励／略带演讲色彩）

> And above all, we need to keep the faith.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** faith 在此可指信心，不必指宗教；日常更直白可说 don't lose hope。

> It's been a difficult year, but I'm trying to keep the faith.（教学自造）

### 30. above all

**意思：** 最重要的是（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And above all, we need to keep the faith.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 31. keep going

**意思：** 继续坚持下去（中性／口语、工作；原素材为演讲）

> We need to keep going.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 第二遍补充：内部动词词块／义务表达骨架。

### 32. I really mean it

**意思：** 我是认真的／我是真心这么说的（口语；原素材为公开演讲）

> I'm so proud to have worked with all of you. I really mean it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202400984/html/DCPD-202400984.htm)

**补充：** 口语确认真诚。

## 2025-01-15 · 拜登：告别讲话

### 1. lay out sth. in great detail

**意思：** 非常详细地说明……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> After 8 months of nonstop negotiation, my administration—by my administration, a cease-fire and a hostage deal has been reached by Israel and Hamas, the elements of which I laid out in great detail in May of this year.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P002](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 2. keep sb. fully informed

**意思：** 让某人充分了解进展（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> That's why I told my team to keep the incoming administration fully informed, because that's how it should be: working together as Americans.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P003](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 3. I've been thinking a lot about sth.

**意思：** 我最近一直在认真考虑某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And I've been thinking a lot about who we are and, maybe more importantly, who we should be.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P004](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 现在完成进行时组织近期思考的说明。

### 4. day after day

**意思：** 日复一日（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Long ago, in New York Harbor, an ironworker installed beam after beam, day after day.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P005](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 5. stand the test of time

**意思：** 经得起时间的考验（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> She was built to sway back and forth to withstand the fury of stormy weather, to stand the test of time, because storms are always coming.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 6. back and forth

**意思：** 来回地（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> She was built to sway back and forth to withstand the fury of stormy weather, to stand the test of time, because storms are always coming.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P007](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 此处实体摇摆，不是观点反复。 同形变体保留：本句实物来回摇摆，另一条是政治争论的反复。

### 7. through good times and tough times

**意思：** 无论顺境还是艰难时期（正式／演讲）

> And yet, through good times and tough times, we've withstood it all.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P008](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 开放搭配框架；演讲强调持续性。

### 8. stand up to sb./sth.

**意思：** 顶住、反抗某人或某种压力（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Ongoing debates about power and the exercise of power, about whether we lead by the example of our power or the power of our example, whether we show the courage to stand up to the abuse of power or we yield to it.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P010](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 与stand up for维护对象不同。

### 9. It may not be perfect, but 从句

**意思：** 它也许不完美，但……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Our system of separation of powers, checks and balances, it may not be perfect, but it's maintained our democracy for nearly 250 years, longer than any other nation in history that's ever tried such a bold experiment.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P012](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 承认限制后保留主要评价。

### 10. It's been the honor of my life to do sth.

**意思：** 做……是我一生的荣幸（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> It's been the honor of my life to see the resilience of essential workers getting us through a once-in-a-century pandemic, the heroism of servicemembers and first responders keeping us safe, the determination of advocates standing up for our rights and our freedoms.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P015](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 郑重感谢；日常可缩小程度，不能把全部当高频寒暄。

### 11. bring sth. to a …-year low

**意思：** 使某项指标降至低点（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And finally doing something to protect our children and our families by passing the most significant gun safety law in 30 years and bringing violent crime to a 50-year low.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P020](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 本句a 50-year low；常用数据说明搭配。原文50-year low，指具体时间范围的最低水平。

### 12. be exposed to sth.

**意思：** 接触／暴露于某物（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Meeting our sacred obligation to over 1 million veterans so far who were exposed to toxic materials and to their families—providing medical care and education benefits and more for their families.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P021](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 此处为有毒物质，不是公开披露。

### 13. for decades to come

**意思：** 在未来几十年里（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> But the seeds are planted, and they'll grow, and they'll bloom for decades to come.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P022](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 未来持续时间；不是过去几十年。

### 14. pull ahead of sb.

**意思：** 领先、超过某人（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And overseas, we've strengthened NATO. Ukraine is still free. And we've pulled ahead of our competition with China. And so much more.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P024](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 竞争中的位置变化。

### 15. wish sb. success

**意思：** 祝某人成功（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> I'm so proud of how much we've accomplished together for the American people. And I wish the incoming administration success, because I want America to succeed.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P025](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 16. I have no doubt that 从句

**意思：** 我毫不怀疑……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> That's why I've upheld my duty to ensure a peaceful and orderly transition of power to ensure we lead by the power of our example. I have no doubt that America is in a position to continue to succeed.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 表达确信，不是弱推测。

### 17. be in a position to do sth.

**意思：** 处于有条件做某事的位置（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> That's why I've upheld my duty to ensure a peaceful and orderly transition of power to ensure we lead by the power of our example. I have no doubt that America is in a position to continue to succeed.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P026](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 18. leave sth. unchecked

**意思：** 任由某事发展而不加约束（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> And this is the dangerous concern—and that's the dangerous concentration of power in the hands of very few ultrawealthy people, and the dangerous consequences if their abuse of power is left unchecked.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P027](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 通常用于令人担忧的趋势。

### 19. take shape

**意思：** 逐渐形成（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Today, an oligarchy is taking shape in America of extreme wealth, power, and influence that literally threatens our entire democracy, our basic rights and freedoms, and a fair shot for everyone to get ahead.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 20. have a fair shot at sth.

**意思：** 有公平的机会争取……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Today, an oligarchy is taking shape in America of extreme wealth, power, and influence that literally threatens our entire democracy, our basic rights and freedoms, and a fair shot for everyone to get ahead.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 原文fair shot后接for everyone；骨架为可迁移候选。

### 21. get ahead

**意思：** 取得进步／改善处境（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Today, an oligarchy is taking shape in America of extreme wealth, power, and influence that literally threatens our entire democracy, our basic rights and freedoms, and a fair shot for everyone to get ahead.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P028](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 22. play by the rules

**意思：** 按规则行事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> They just made the wealthy pay the by—play by the rules everybody else had to.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P030](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 原文口误pay the by—play保留，不收口误为模板。

### 23. earn sb.'s fair share

**意思：** 获得自己应得的份额（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Workers won rights to earn their fair share.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P030](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 24. be at stake

**意思：** 处于利害攸关之中；可能失去（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> So much is at stake. Right now the existential threat of climate change has never been clearer. Just look across the country, from California to North Carolina.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P033](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 25. We don't have to choose between A and B

**意思：** 我们不必在A与B之间二选一（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You know, we've proven we don't have to choose between protecting the environment and growing the economy.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P035](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 澄清两者并非不可兼得。

### 26. be bullied into doing sth.

**意思：** 被胁迫去做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We must not be bullied into sacrificing the future, the future of our children and our grandchildren.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P036](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 27. There is no time to waste

**意思：** 没有时间可浪费（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We must keep pushing forward and push faster. There is no time to waste.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P036](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 催促行动的套语。

### 28. pose a danger to sb./sth.

**意思：** 对……构成危险（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You know, his farewell address, President Eisenhower spoke of the dangers of the military- industrial complex. He warned us then about, and I quote, "the potential for the disastrous rise of misplaced power," end of quote. Six days—six decades later, I'm equally concerned about the potential rise of a tech-industrial complex that could pose real dangers for our country as well.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P038](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 29. give up on sth.

**意思：** 不再尝试／放弃某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Americans are being buried under an avalanche of misinformation and disinformation enabling the abuse of power. The free press is crumbling. Editors are disappearing. Social media is giving up on fact-checking. The truth is smothered by lies told for power and for profit.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P039](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 30. hold sb. accountable

**意思：** 要求某人为其行为负责（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We must hold the social platforms accountable to protect our children, our families, and our very democracy from the abuse of power.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P040](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 31. of our time / of all time

**意思：** 我们这个时代的／有史以来的（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Meanwhile, artificial intelligence is the most consequential technology of our time, perhaps of all time. Nothing offers more profound possibilities and risks for our economy and our security, our society, it's our very—for humanity.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P041](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 比较时间范围不同。

### 32. have the potential to do sth.

**意思：** 有潜力／可能做到……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Artificial intelligence even has the potential to help us answer my call to end cancer as we know it. But unless safeguards are in place, AI could spawn new threats to our rights, our way of life, to our privacy, how we work, and how we protect our Nation.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P042](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 33. be in place

**意思：** 安排、措施已到位（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Artificial intelligence even has the potential to help us answer my call to end cancer as we know it. But unless safeguards are in place, AI could spawn new threats to our rights, our way of life, to our privacy, how we work, and how we protect our Nation.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P042](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 34. ban sb. from doing sth.

**意思：** 禁止某人做某事（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We need to ban Members of Congress from trading stock while they're in the Congress.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P048](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 35. be immune from sth.

**意思：** 免于……；不受……约束（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> We need to amend the Constitution to make clear that no President—no President—is immune from crimes that he or she commits while in office. The President's power is not limited—it's not absolute, and it shouldn't be.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P049](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 本句为犯罪法律责任；非医学免疫。

### 36. feel like 从句

**意思：** 感觉／觉得……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Participating in our democracy becomes exhausting and even disillusioning, and people don't feel like they have a fair shot.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P050](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 原句people don't feel like they have，非feel like doing想做。

### 37. be entitled to sth.

**意思：** 有权获得……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> A fair shot is what makes America, America. Everyone is entitled to a fair shot—not a guarantee, but just a fair shot, an even playing field—going as far as your hard work and talent can take you. We can never lose that essential truth—remain who we are.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P052](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 38. an even playing field

**意思：** 公平的竞争环境（正式／演讲）

> Everyone is entitled to a fair shot—not a guarantee, but just a fair shot, an even playing field—going as far as your hard work and talent can take you.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P052](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 常见level playing field的本句变体；待跨语料核验后正式教学。

### 39. from modest beginnings

**意思：** 从普通／并不优越的起点出发（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Only in America do we believe anything is possible, like a kid with a stutter from modest beginnings in Scranton, Pennsylvania, and Claymont, Delaware, sitting behind the—this desk in the Oval Office as President of the United States.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P053](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 叙事，不等于贫困。

### 40. remind sb. of sth.

**意思：** 使某人想起……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> Upstairs in the Residence of the White House, I've walked by a painting of the Statue of Liberty I don't know how many times. In the painting, there are several workers climbing on the outstretched arm of the statue that holds the torch. It reminds me every day I pass it of the story and soul of our Nation and the power of the American people.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P055](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 41. be known as sth.

**意思：** 被称为……（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> There's a story of a veteran—this is—a veteran, a son of an immigrant, whose job it was to climb that torch and polish the amber panes so rays of light could reach out as far as possible. He was known as the "keeper of the flame."

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P056](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 不同于known for因某特点出名。

### 42. closer than we think

**意思：** 比我们想象的更近（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> But what I believe is the America of our dreams is always closer than we think.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P059](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 可用于接近目标的评价。

### 43. Let me close by doing sth.

**意思：** 让我以……结束（正式／演讲）

> Let me close by stating my gratitude to so many people.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P060](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 正式讲话结束语。

### 44. become like family

**意思：** 变得像家人一样（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> She and Doug have become like family.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P062](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 候选：保留本句语境；尚未作跨语料频率与教学用法审校。

### 45. the love of sb.'s life

**意思：** 一生挚爱（中性／观点表达、工作讨论；原素材为正式演讲）

> You are the love of my life and lifes of my love.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P063](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 只引用正常词块；后续lifes of my love为口误，不作模板。

### 46. stand guard

**意思：** 守卫、保持警戒（正式／演讲）

> Now it's your turn to stand guard.

**来源：** [Joe Biden · P065](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/DCPD-202500078/html/DCPD-202500078.htm)

**补充：** 演讲中作守护价值的比喻。
